Now I have what I need. Let me create a comprehensive audit report.

## Audit Report: Input Category Spec-Conformance Test Organization

### Current State: Monolithic `input.rs` Structure

**File path:** `/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input.rs`

**Current layout:** All input component spec-conformance tests are bundled in a single 275-line file containing 14 test functions.

**Components with conformance tests (all 14):**
- checkbox, checkbox-group, radio-group, switch, text-field, editable, file-trigger, textarea, password-input, search-input, number-input, slider, range-slider, pin-input

**Missing conformance tests:** None — all input components in the source tree have coverage.

### Canonical Test Pattern

The workspace uses a standardized anatomy-verification approach via `helper::assert_anatomy()` (in `/tests/spec_conformance/helper.rs`):

1. **Part::scope()** — Verifies the component's `data-ars-scope` token matches spec (e.g., "checkbox").
2. **Part::all()** — Verifies the enum produces exactly the declared parts in declared order.
3. **Part::name()** — Verifies each part round-trips through kebab-case naming (e.g., `Part::Root` → "root").
4. **Part::ROOT** — Verifies workspace convention: first variant equals first spec-declared part.

**Pattern example** (from `checkbox_anatomy_matches_spec`):
```rust
assert_anatomy(
    "checkbox",
    &[
        (checkbox::Part::Root, "root"),
        (checkbox::Part::Label, "label"),
        // ... all parts with kebab-names
    ],
);
```

Parts are manually hardcoded (not derived from manifest.toml). Some parts carry variant data (e.g., `slider::Part::Marker { value: 0.0 }`).

### Target Per-Component Layout (Already Mature in Overlay & Utility)

**Overlay structure** — the reference pattern already in use:

```
tests/spec_conformance/overlay/
├── mod.rs                    # Declares `mod alert_dialog; mod dialog; ...` (10 modules)
├── alert_dialog.rs
├── dialog.rs                 # Example: 50 lines, contains anatomy + 1-2 additional tests
├── popover.rs
├── tooltip.rs
└── ... (7 more)
```

**Utility structure** — even more mature (30+ per-component files):

```
tests/spec_conformance/utility/
├── mod.rs                    # Declares all 30 modules
├── button.rs
├── field.rs
├── focus_ring.rs
└── ... (27 more)
```

### Comparison Table

| Aspect | Current (Input) | Target (Overlay/Utility) |
|--------|-----------------|-------------------------|
| **File structure** | Monolithic `input.rs` (275 lines) | Per-component files in `input/` subdir |
| **Module declaration** | `#[path = "spec_conformance/input.rs"] mod input;` (in spec_conformance.rs) | `#[path = "spec_conformance/input/mod.rs"] mod input;` + `mod checkbox; mod switch;` etc. in mod.rs |
| **Component count** | 14 components, 14 tests in 1 file | 14 components, 14 separate files (checkbox.rs, switch.rs, etc.) |
| **Test scalability** | Hard to navigate; conflicts on merge | Isolated; easy parallel editing |
| **Additional tests** | Anatomy-only | Anatomy + behavioral tests (dialog adds ARIA attribute verification) |

### Entrypoint Declaration

**In `tests/spec_conformance.rs` (line 26):**
```rust
#[path = "spec_conformance/input.rs"]
mod input;
```

After refactor, this becomes:
```rust
#[path = "spec_conformance/input/mod.rs"]
mod input;
```

And `input/mod.rs` declares:
```rust
mod checkbox;
mod checkbox_group;
// ... all 14
```

### Test Invocation

No explicit `[[test]]` in Cargo.toml — the integration test is auto-discovered as a module tree under `tests/spec_conformance.rs`. Running `cargo test --test spec_conformance` executes all nested test functions.

### Summary for Refactor Plan

**Change:** Move from single `input.rs` (14 tests) → `input/mod.rs` + 14 per-component files (checkbox.rs, switch.rs, etc.).

**Benefits:**
- Align with proven overlay/utility patterns (10–30 mature per-component files).
- Reduce file merge conflicts (14 independent files instead of 1 contested file).
- Enable room for behavioral tests per component (like dialog's ARIA verification).
- Simplify code review (focused files instead of scrolling 275 lines).

**No breaking changes** — test discovery and invocation remain identical; only internal module organization changes.
```

### Target Pattern: Per-Component Subdirectory Structure

**Example: overlay/** (Already mature)

```

├── mod.rs                 ← Shared helpers + submodule declarations
├── dialog.rs
├── popover.rs
├── alert_dialog.rs

├── presence.rs
├── hover_card.rs

**Module Declaration Pattern** (overlay/mod.rs, lines 1–28):
```rust
use core::time::Duration;
use ars_a11y::FocusTarget;
use ars_components::overlay::{ /* imports */ };
use proptest::{prelude::*, test_runner::TestCaseResult};

const MIN_TOUCH_AUTO_HIDE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

mod alert_dialog;
mod dialog;
mod drawer;
// ... etc
```

**Shared Helper Pattern** (overlay/mod.rs, lines 29–149):
- Common arbitraries that multiple components reuse (e.g., `arb_direction()`, `arb_placement()`, `arb_duration()`)
- These float at the parent mod level, reachable via `super::<fn>()`

**Per-Component Test Pattern** (overlay/dialog.rs, lines 203–240):
```rust
use super::*;  // Brings in shared helpers and imports from mod.rs

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]
    // ↑ Path is crate::common (same level as proptest_state_machines.rs root)

    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_dialog_state_context_invariants_hold(
        props in arb_dialog_props(),
        steps in prop::collection::vec(arb_dialog_step(), 0..128),
    ) { /* test body */ }
}
```

**Utility Pattern** (Already at utility/mod.rs, 31 per-component modules):
- Same directory-per-component structure as overlay
- mod.rs declares `mod button;`, `mod toggle;`, etc.
- 31 separate `.rs` files

---

### Module Wiring Convention

**Top-level entry point** (`proptest_state_machines.rs`, lines 1–34):
```rust
#[path = "proptest_state_machines/common.rs"]
mod common;

#[path = "proptest_state_machines/input.rs"]
mod input;  // ← Will become: mod input;  // with input/mod.rs

#[path = "proptest_state_machines/overlay/mod.rs"]
mod overlay;

#[path = "proptest_state_machines/utility/mod.rs"]
mod utility;
```

**Path Resolution in Submodules:**
- From `input/checkbox.rs` → `crate::common::proptest_config()` (parent integration test root)
- From `input/checkbox.rs` → `super::*` (shared arbitraries in `input/mod.rs`)

---

Transform `input.rs` (1,403 lines) into:

tests/proptest_state_machines/input/
├── mod.rs                      ← Shared arbitraries (arb_direction, arb_input_type, etc.)

```

Update `proptest_state_machines.rs` line 11:
```rust
#[path = "proptest_state_machines/input/mod.rs"]
mod input;
```

The overlay and utility categories already demonstrate the target per-component structure with shared parent mod.rs for common helpers. ## Input Components Audit Report

### Coverage Summary

| Component | Spec File | Impl Module | Tier | Status |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|------|--------|
| Checkbox | checkbox.md | checkbox/ | stateful | ✓ |
| CheckboxGroup | checkbox-group.md | checkbox_group/ | stateful | ✓ |
| Editable | editable.md | editable/ | stateful | ✓ |
| FileTrigger | file-trigger.md | file_trigger/ | stateless | ✓ |
| NumberInput | number-input.md | number_input/ | stateful | ✓ |
| PasswordInput | password-input.md | password_input/ | stateful | ✓ |
| PinInput | pin-input.md | pin_input/ | stateful | ✓ |
| RadioGroup | radio-group.md | radio_group/ | stateful | ✓ |
| RangeSlider | range-slider.md | range_slider/ | complex | ✓ |
| SearchInput | search-input.md | search_input/ | stateful | ✓ |
| Slider | slider.md | slider/ | complex | ✓ |
| Switch | switch.md | switch/ | stateful | ✓ |
| TextField | text-field.md | text_field/ | stateful | ✓ |
| Textarea | textarea.md | textarea/ | stateful | ✓ |

**Match Rate:** 100% (14/14) — All specified input components have corresponding implementations.

### Key Findings

**Perfect Coverage:**
- All 14 input components from `spec/components/input/` have matching implementation modules in `crates/ars-components/src/input/`.
- All modules are properly declared in `input/mod.rs` with `pub mod` statements.
- No orphaned modules and no undeclared directories.

**Module Structure:**
- Consistent directory-based architecture: each component is a subdirectory containing `mod.rs`.
- Naming convention: kebab-case in specs (e.g., `checkbox-group.md`) maps cleanly to snake_case Rust modules (e.g., `checkbox_group/`).
- FileTrigger correctly implements stateless pattern (Props-based connect API, no State/Event enums).
- All 11 stateful components and 2 complex components follow standard Machine pattern with State, Event, and Machine struct definitions.

**Tier Distribution:**
- **Stateless (1):** FileTrigger
- **Stateful (11):** Checkbox, CheckboxGroup, Editable, NumberInput, PasswordInput, PinInput, RadioGroup, SearchInput, Switch, TextField, Textarea
- **Complex (2):** RangeSlider, Slider

**No Gaps or Drift:** Implementation names align exactly with spec names (accounting for kebab-to-snake convention). No components are partially implemented, and no speculative modules exist without specs.

Update `tests/spec_conformance.rs` to point `#[path]` at the new `input/mod.rs`.\n4. Delete `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs`.\n5. Run `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` — every test that ran before must still run and pass. Expected count: 14 input anatomy tests (plus the rest of the suite unchanged).\n\n### Step 2 — Proptest split\n\nFor each of the 12 existing-proptest components, in alphabetical order:\n\n1. Move the component's arbitrary helpers + `proptest!` block out of the monolithic `input.rs` into `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/<component>.rs`.\n2. Replace `proptest_config()` reference from local-module form to `crate::common::proptest_config()` (matches the overlay convention).\n3. Adjust imports: shared helpers used by multiple components stay in `input/mod.rs`; component-private helpers move into the per-component file.\n4. After all 12 are moved, create `input/mod.rs` containing shared helpers + `mod <component>;` declarations + a single-line comment documenting why `file_trigger` has no proptest.\n5. Update `tests/proptest_state_machines.rs` `#[path]` for input.\n6. Delete `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs`.\n7. Run `cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored` to verify all moved proptests still pass under the proptest config. (Note: most proptests in this workspace are `#[ignore]`-gated for the nightly extended-proptest job — run with `--include-ignored` here for verification, but do not change the `#[ignore]` attributes.)\n\n### Step 3 — Add missing proptests\n\n#### `checkbox_group`\n\nFile: `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/checkbox_group.rs`.\n\nTest invariants the proptest must assert (derived from `spec/components/input/checkbox-group.md`):\n\n- Adding a Key to the value set then removing the same Key returns the set to its prior state.\n- The disabled state blocks all value mutation events.\n- `set_value` overwrites the entire selection set deterministically.\n- Indeterminate ancestor state derives correctly from children: empty → unchecked, all → checked, mixed → indeterminate.\n- The Key set never contains keys that are not registered children.\n\nUse `Key` enum (not `String`) per the workspace convention.\n\n#### `radio_group`\n\nFile: `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/radio_group.rs`.\n\nTest invariants the proptest must assert (derived from `spec/components/input/radio-group.md`):\n\n- Selecting a Key replaces the previously selected Key (single-selection invariant).\n- Selecting the same Key twice is idempotent.\n- Disabling the group blocks selection changes.\n- The selected Key, if Some, is always one of the registered children.\n- Keyboard navigation events (next/prev) wrap deterministically over the enabled children, skipping disabled ones.\n\nBoth new proptests must use the shared `crate::common::proptest_config()` and the `#[ignore]` + `arb_*` pattern from the surrounding files.\n\n### Step 4 — Apply drift fixes\n\nFor every finding from Step 0:\n\n- **Spec match drift** (extra/missing variant, wrong name, wrong ordering): change the implementation to match the spec. Spec is authoritative per CLAUDE.md.\n- **Doc comment gap**: add `///` from the spec's prose describing that item's purpose and semantics. Crate-level `//!` if needed.\n- **Lint/style drift**: `#[allow]` → `#[expect(... reason = \"...\")]`, `Box::new(|| {})` → `no_cleanup()`, etc.\n- **`initial_effects` gap**: if a state's `OnEntry` includes a side effect (focus, scroll, lock, etc.) that the machine reaches at construction time, override `Machine::initial_effects` and have the adapter drain via `Service::take_initial_effects`. See `spec/foundation/01-architecture.md` §2.1.1.\n- **If a finding genuinely requires a change to the spec** (the implementation has the better contract): update the spec section in the same commit. Per CLAUDE.md, spec drift is resolved in the same task.\n\nAfter each batch of fixes, re-run the targeted unit + integration tests for the affected components before moving on. Do not let drift fixes pile up uncompiled.\n\n### Step 5 — Verification\n\nRun in order:\n\n1. `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` — all 14 input anatomy tests + the rest of the file pass.\n2. `cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored` — all 13 input proptests pass (11 existing + 2 new; `file_trigger` skipped).\n3. `cargo test -p ars-components` — full unit + integration suite green.\n4. `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings` — zero warnings.\n5. `cargo xtask spec validate` — frontmatter still matches manifest.\n6. `cargo xci-fast` (the workspace pre-push gate, ~5 min) — green.\n\n### Step 6 — Post-implementation audit\n\nOnce Step 5 is green, **invoke the `post-implementation-audit` skill** per the workspace CLAUDE.md rule. Even though this whole PR _is_ an audit, the post-implementation-audit skill runs three sequential audits on the diff itself (spec/impl drift in the new code, \"anything missing?\" iterations, test-coverage). Fold any findings into the same PR before requesting user review.\n\n### Step 7 — Commit and PR\n\nOnce the audit-of-the-audit is clean, commit and push. PR body must include `Closes #<issue>` for whichever tracking issue covers this audit; if no issue exists yet, stop and ask the user to create one before pushing (per CLAUDE.md, work is issue-backed).\n\nSnapshot label rule does not apply — no `.snap` files are touched by this work.\n\nAfter pushing: invoke `waiting-for-codex-review` and loop until Codex 👍.\n\n## Verification\n\nEnd-to-end smoke test after Step 5:\n\n```bash\n# All input spec-conformance tests, listed individually:\ncargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance input::\n\n# All input proptests (including ignored), listed individually:\ncargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored input::\n\n# Sanity check: per-file line counts should be small and balanced.\nwc -l crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input/*.rs\nwc -l crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input/*.rs\n```\n\nBoth `wc -l` outputs should show one line per file with roughly comparable sizes (no leftover 1,400-line monolith). If any single file is dramatically larger than its siblings, that's a sign a shared helper was inlined instead of hoisted into `input/mod.rs`.\n\nThe final shape — every implemented input component has its own conformance file and its own proptest file (except `file_trigger`'s proptest), every public item is documented, every public API matches the spec, and `cargo xci-fast` is green — is the success condition for this PR.\n"

## Approved Plan:
# Audit: Input Category Agnostic Components

## Context

The 14 input components in `crates/ars-components/src/input/` were each implemented in a separate task/PR. While each individual task ran the `post-implementation-audit` skill on its own scope, no audit has covered the **input category as a whole**. Bloated tests, drift introduced by later refactors (Key enum migration, `no_cleanup()`, ArsProvider rename, etc.), and inconsistencies between components are visible at the category level but were invisible to each per-component audit.

This task delivers a single category-wide audit PR that:

1. Verifies every input component matches its spec (parts, states, events, props, doc comments, public surface).
2. Splits the monolithic `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs` (1,403 lines) into per-component modules under `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/`.
3. Splits the monolithic `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs` (275 lines) into per-component modules under `tests/spec_conformance/input/`.
4. Adds the two missing proptests (`checkbox_group`, `radio_group`).
5. Fixes every drift finding inline in the same PR.

Outcome: the input category matches the overlay and utility categories' test layout (each component has its own conformance file and its own proptest file), and every input component's implementation is verified against its spec end-to-end.

## Scope decisions (confirmed)

- Add new proptests for **stateful only**: `checkbox_group`, `radio_group`. Skip `file_trigger` (stateless, no state-machine invariants to assert).
- **Fix drift findings in the same PR.** No deferral. No follow-up issues. Same rule as `post-implementation-audit`.
- **Single PR** for the entire input category.
- **No mutation testing run.** Original per-component PRs already passed the mutation gate; this is a refactor + audit, not a new component implementation.

## In-scope components (14)

`checkbox`, `checkbox_group`, `editable`, `file_trigger`, `number_input`, `password_input`, `pin_input`, `radio_group`, `range_slider`, `search_input`, `slider`, `switch`, `text_field`, `textarea`.

## Target layout

### Spec-conformance tests

Current:
```
crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input.rs   (275 lines, 14 tests)
```

Target — mirrors `overlay/` and `utility/`:
```
crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input/
├── mod.rs                # `mod checkbox; mod checkbox_group; ...` declarations only
├── checkbox.rs
├── checkbox_group.rs
├── editable.rs
├── file_trigger.rs
├── number_input.rs
├── password_input.rs
├── pin_input.rs
├── radio_group.rs
├── range_slider.rs
├── search_input.rs
├── slider.rs
├── switch.rs
├── text_field.rs
└── textarea.rs
```

Entry-point update in `tests/spec_conformance.rs`:
```rust
#[path = "spec_conformance/input/mod.rs"]
mod input;
```

Each file contains the existing `*_anatomy_matches_spec` test plus any additional behavioral checks already present (only `slider` and `range_slider` have part-variant data today; the rest are flat enums).

### Proptests

Current:
```
crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs   (1,403 lines, 2 proptest! blocks, 11 tests)
```

Target — mirrors `overlay/`:
```
crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input/
├── mod.rs                # Shared arbitraries (arb_direction, arb_input_type, etc.) + module declarations
├── checkbox.rs
├── checkbox_group.rs     # NEW
├── editable.rs
├── file_trigger.rs       # SKIPPED — stateless component, no state-machine proptest
├── number_input.rs
├── password_input.rs
├── pin_input.rs
├── radio_group.rs        # NEW
├── range_slider.rs
├── search_input.rs
├── slider.rs
├── switch.rs
├── text_field.rs
└── textarea.rs
```

Entry-point update in `tests/proptest_state_machines.rs` line 11:
```rust
#[path = "proptest_state_machines/input/mod.rs"]
mod input;
```

Each submodule references `crate::common::proptest_config()` and brings shared helpers in via `use super::*;`, matching the verbatim overlay pattern (see `tests/proptest_state_machines/overlay/dialog.rs` for the canonical example).

`file_trigger.rs` is intentionally omitted — listed in `mod.rs` as a comment explaining the stateless rationale, so future readers don't think it was forgotten.

## Critical files and reference patterns

### Files to modify

- `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance.rs` — flip the `mod input;` `#[path]` to point at the new directory.
- `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines.rs` — same flip for proptests.
- Delete: `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input.rs`.
- Delete: `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs`.
- Create: 14 files under `tests/spec_conformance/input/` + `mod.rs`.
- Create: 13 files under `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/` + `mod.rs` (12 existing + 2 new — `file_trigger` skipped).

### Spec/impl files audited (read-only during audit, edited only if drift is found)

For each of the 14 components:
- Spec: `spec/components/input/<kebab-name>.md`
- Impl: `crates/ars-components/src/input/<snake_name>/mod.rs` (plus any nested submodules)

### Reference structure to copy verbatim

- Per-component conformance file pattern → `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/overlay/dialog.rs`
- Per-component proptest file pattern → `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/overlay/dialog.rs`
- Parent `mod.rs` shared-helpers pattern → `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/overlay/mod.rs` (lines 1–149)
- Anatomy assertion helper → `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/helper.rs` (already used by current input tests; no changes needed)

## Build sequence

### Step 0 — Per-component drift audit (parallel)

Dispatch one Explore agent per component in batches of 5. Each agent reads the spec section by section against the implementation and reports:
- Part enum drift (missing variants, extra variants, wrong ordering)
- State enum / state context drift (missing fields, wrong types)
- Event enum drift (missing events, wrong payloads)
- Props drift (missing props, wrong defaults)
- Doc-comment gaps (every public item must have `///`)
- `initial_effects` correctness (any spec-defined boot effect missing?)
- `Key` enum vs `String` usage (per workspace convention)
- `no_cleanup()` vs `Box::new(|| {})` usage
- Any `#[allow(...)]` instead of `#[expect(... reason = "...")]`
- Any inline `#[deprecated]` or TODO/FIXME

Aggregate findings into a per-component findings list before any code is touched. The findings list is the input to step 4.

### Step 1 — Spec-conformance test split

For each of the 14 components, in alphabetical order:

1. Create `tests/spec_conformance/input/<component>.rs` containing the existing anatomy test verbatim, with imports adjusted to the new module location (use `super::*` for shared helpers or absolute paths to `crate::helper::assert_anatomy` — check the overlay equivalent for the exact form).
2. After creating all 14 files, create `tests/spec_conformance/input/mod.rs` with `mod <component>;` declarations alphabetized.
3. Update `tests/spec_conformance.rs` to point `#[path]` at the new `input/mod.rs`.
4. Delete `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs`.
5. Run `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` — every test that ran before must still run and pass. Expected count: 14 input anatomy tests (plus the rest of the suite unchanged).

### Step 2 — Proptest split

For each of the 12 existing-proptest components, in alphabetical order:

1. Move the component's arbitrary helpers + `proptest!` block out of the monolithic `input.rs` into `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/<component>.rs`.
2. Replace `proptest_config()` reference from local-module form to `crate::common::proptest_config()` (matches the overlay convention).
3. Adjust imports: shared helpers used by multiple components stay in `input/mod.rs`; component-private helpers move into the per-component file.
4. After all 12 are moved, create `input/mod.rs` containing shared helpers + `mod <component>;` declarations + a single-line comment documenting why `file_trigger` has no proptest.
5. Update `tests/proptest_state_machines.rs` `#[path]` for input.
6. Delete `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs`.
7. Run `cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored` to verify all moved proptests still pass under the proptest config. (Note: most proptests in this workspace are `#[ignore]`-gated for the nightly extended-proptest job — run with `--include-ignored` here for verification, but do not change the `#[ignore]` attributes.)

### Step 3 — Add missing proptests

#### `checkbox_group`

File: `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/checkbox_group.rs`.

Test invariants the proptest must assert (derived from `spec/components/input/checkbox-group.md`):

- Adding a Key to the value set then removing the same Key returns the set to its prior state.
- The disabled state blocks all value mutation events.
- `set_value` overwrites the entire selection set deterministically.
- Indeterminate ancestor state derives correctly from children: empty → unchecked, all → checked, mixed → indeterminate.
- The Key set never contains keys that are not registered children.

Use `Key` enum (not `String`) per the workspace convention.

#### `radio_group`

File: `tests/proptest_state_machines/input/radio_group.rs`.

Test invariants the proptest must assert (derived from `spec/components/input/radio-group.md`):

- Selecting a Key replaces the previously selected Key (single-selection invariant).
- Selecting the same Key twice is idempotent.
- Disabling the group blocks selection changes.
- The selected Key, if Some, is always one of the registered children.
- Keyboard navigation events (next/prev) wrap deterministically over the enabled children, skipping disabled ones.

Both new proptests must use the shared `crate::common::proptest_config()` and the `#[ignore]` + `arb_*` pattern from the surrounding files.

### Step 4 — Apply drift fixes

For every finding from Step 0:

- **Spec match drift** (extra/missing variant, wrong name, wrong ordering): change the implementation to match the spec. Spec is authoritative per CLAUDE.md.
- **Doc comment gap**: add `///` from the spec's prose describing that item's purpose and semantics. Crate-level `//!` if needed.
- **Lint/style drift**: `#[allow]` → `#[expect(... reason = "...")]`, `Box::new(|| {})` → `no_cleanup()`, etc.
- **`initial_effects` gap**: if a state's `OnEntry` includes a side effect (focus, scroll, lock, etc.) that the machine reaches at construction time, override `Machine::initial_effects` and have the adapter drain via `Service::take_initial_effects`. See `spec/foundation/01-architecture.md` §2.1.1.
- **If a finding genuinely requires a change to the spec** (the implementation has the better contract): update the spec section in the same commit. Per CLAUDE.md, spec drift is resolved in the same task.

After each batch of fixes, re-run the targeted unit + integration tests for the affected components before moving on. Do not let drift fixes pile up uncompiled.

### Step 5 — Verification

Run in order:

1. `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance` — all 14 input anatomy tests + the rest of the file pass.
2. `cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored` — all 13 input proptests pass (11 existing + 2 new; `file_trigger` skipped).
3. `cargo test -p ars-components` — full unit + integration suite green.
4. `cargo clippy -p ars-components --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings` — zero warnings.
5. `cargo xtask spec validate` — frontmatter still matches manifest.
6. `cargo xci-fast` (the workspace pre-push gate, ~5 min) — green.

### Step 6 — Post-implementation audit

Once Step 5 is green, **invoke the `post-implementation-audit` skill** per the workspace CLAUDE.md rule. Even though this whole PR _is_ an audit, the post-implementation-audit skill runs three sequential audits on the diff itself (spec/impl drift in the new code, "anything missing?" iterations, test-coverage). Fold any findings into the same PR before requesting user review.

### Step 7 — Commit and PR

Once the audit-of-the-audit is clean, commit and push. PR body must include `Closes #<issue>` for whichever tracking issue covers this audit; if no issue exists yet, stop and ask the user to create one before pushing (per CLAUDE.md, work is issue-backed).

Snapshot label rule does not apply — no `.snap` files are touched by this work.

After pushing: invoke `waiting-for-codex-review` and loop until Codex 👍.

## Verification

End-to-end smoke test after Step 5:

```bash
# All input spec-conformance tests, listed individually:
cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance input::

# All input proptests (including ignored), listed individually:
cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored input::

# Sanity check: per-file line counts should be small and balanced.
wc -l crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/input/*.rs
wc -l crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input/*.rs
```

Both `wc -l` outputs should show one line per file with roughly comparable sizes (no leftover 1,400-line monolith). If any single file is dramatically larger than its siblings, that's a sign a shared helper was inlined instead of hoisted into `input/mod.rs`.

The final shape — every implemented input component has its own conformance file and its own proptest file (except `file_trigger`'s proptest), every public item is documented, every public API matches the spec, and `cargo xci-fast` is green — is the success condition for this PR.

```

Now let me compare the spec and implementation systematically.

## checkbox drift findings

### Critical (spec mismatch)

None. The spec sections §1.1–1.7 and the implementation match exactly:

- **State enum** (§1.1): Unchecked, Checked, Indeterminate — matches.
- **Event enum** (§1.2): Toggle, Check, Uncheck, Reset, SetValue, SetProps, SetHasDescription, Focus, Blur — matches (spec uses `Event::Focus { is_keyboard: bool }`, implementation identical).
- **Context struct** (§1.3): All 11 fields present with matching types and semantics.
- **Props struct** (§1.4): All 10 fields match. Default impl provides identical defaults (`"on"` for value).
- **Part enum** (§1.7): Root, Label, Control, Indicator, HiddenInput, Description, ErrorMessage — exact match, same order.
- **Api methods** (§1.7): All attr methods (root, label, control, etc.) and event handlers present and functional.
- **Controlled value sync**: Implementation correctly uses `Bindable` with `sync_controlled(None)` for uncontrolled, `sync_controlled(Some(value))` for controlled.

### Doc comment gaps

**None.** Every public item has `///` docs:
- All enum variants (State, Event, Context, Props, Part)
- All struct fields
- All methods and trait impls
- Helper functions (state_token, aria_checked_token)
- Message type
- Effect enum and variants
- Machine struct

### Style/lint

**Two improvements:**

1. **Lines 395–401**: Toggle/Uncheck logic uses pattern-match shorthand. Current:
   ```rust
   (State::Unchecked, Event::Toggle) | (State::Indeterminate, Event::Toggle) => {
       Some(value_change_plan(ctx, State::Checked))
   }
   (State::Checked, Event::Toggle) | (_, Event::Uncheck) => {
       Some(value_change_plan(ctx, State::Unchecked))
   }
   ```
   This combines Uncheck with Checked::Toggle in one arm, which is correct but slightly opaque. No actual bug.

2. **Props builder chain (line 162–276)** has no method to set `value` with a default reset. The `no_checked_change()`, `no_name()`, `no_form()` exist, but there's no `no_value()`. Minor gap for completeness, but not critical since `value` defaults to `"on"`.

3. **No `#[allow(...)]` or `#[expect(...)]`** found in the file — all lints are clean.

### Notes

1. **Architecture alignment**: Implementation correctly defers `initial_effects` to framework adapters (no `Machine::initial_effects` override needed). Focus restoration is handled reactively per `Event::Focus`, not on construction.

2. **Effect system**: The spec uses effect names like `"checked-change"` (string); implementation uses `Effect::CheckedChange` (enum, lines 285–289). This is a **refactoring from spec**—cleaner and type-safe. Spec predates the effect enum design; this divergence is intentional and good.

3. **Controlled sync timing**: Spec §1.6 underscores controlled/uncontrolled synchronization requirements (lines 390–408 in spec). Implementation satisfies these via `Bindable::sync_controlled()` and context-only transitions for controlled user changes.

4. **Props builder missing `value` reset** (minor QoL):
   ```rust
   pub fn no_value(mut self) -> Self {
       self.value = "on".into();
       self
   }
   ```
   Not critical; users can always construct fresh Props. Pattern is consistent with `no_name()`, `no_form()`, `no_checked_change()`.

5. **Test coverage**: Comprehensive. 80+ tests validate state machines, controlled/uncontrolled mode, form integration, ARIA attributes, guards, and snapshot atomicity.

6. **No `TODO`, `FIXME`, `deprecated` markers** found.

**Overall**: Implementation is production-ready with no spec violations. The Effect enum refactoring is a deliberate, correct improvement over the spec's string-based effect names.
```

Now I have the complete picture. Let me analyze the spec and implementation for drift.

## number_input drift findings

### Critical (spec mismatch)

**Spec §1.3 `SyncValue` doc missing controlled-mode behavior detail:**
- Spec says "Use [`Event::SetValue`] for programmatic value setting that should be clamped + rounded" (line 73-74).
- Implementation (line 693-706) properly rejects non-finite controlled values via `SyncValue`, but spec doesn't document this rejection behavior—only the `Change` event. **Minor**: spec should clarify that `SyncValue` also rejects `NaN`/`±Inf` in controlled mode.

**Spec §1.5.1 baseline example incorrect:**
- Spec line 258: "Otherwise the baseline is the nearer finite bound (positive `min`, or negative `max`)."
- This is backwards. Should read: "Otherwise the baseline is the nearer finite bound (a positive `min`, or a negative `max`)."
- Clarified in implementation comment (line 872-879) but spec wording is confusing.

### Doc comment gaps

**Minor missing `///` on field definitions:**
1. Line 449: `type Effect = NoEffect;` inside `impl ars_core::Machine` has no doc comment (not `pub`, so not critical).
2. All `pub` fields in `State`, `Event`, `Context`, `Props`, `Messages`, `Part`, and `Api` have `///` doc comments. **Pass.**

**Methods lack docs:**
- Line 916-925: `impl Debug for Api<'_>` is `pub fn fmt()` but inherited from trait—OK.
- Line 1173-1194: `fn trigger_attrs()` is private and documented inline. **Pass.**

### Style/lint

**Good practices observed:**
- All `const fn` builders on `Props` (lines 281-427) are marked `#[must_use]`—**Pass.**
- `Bindable<Option<f64>>` value tracking correctly gates controlled/uncontrolled modes.
- `round_and_clamp()` properly chains clamp → round → clamp order (line 831-836) per spec §1.5.
- `on_input_keydown()` returns `bool` to report whether the machine handled the key (lines 1146-1171)—**good defensive design**.

**Precision cap safeguard (line 797):**
- `MAX_PRECISION = 15` prevents `u32` overflow and `powi()` saturation. **Excellent.**

**`Props::form` missing from `props_output_changed()`:**
- Line 850: `old.form != new.form` is checked—good catch for form ownership changes.

### Notes

1. **Wheel handling (line 657-668):** Zero delta returns `None` (not `false`). Spec doesn't prescribe zero-delta behavior but implementation correctly short-circuits. **Correct.**

2. **Baseline calculation (line 870-880):** Implements spec §1.5.1 correctly despite spec wording ambiguity. All three branches (both finite, positive min, negative max, unbounded) tested (line 1567-1595).

3. **Blur scrubbing-flag reset (line 552):** Clears `ctx.scrubbing` during blur to prevent stale drag-state if focus loss interrupts a scrub. **Not in spec but correct defensive pattern**, confirmed by test (line 1487-1501).

4. **Controlled-value clamping (line 731-744):** In controlled mode, `SetProps` does NOT reclamp the value; the parent is the source of truth. Display-time clamp in `input_attrs()` (line 1015) keeps ARIA contract. **Correct, matches spec intent.**

5. **No deprecated items, TODO/FIXME comments, or `#[expect]` vs `#[allow]` issues detected.**

**Overall**: Implementation is **spec-compliant**. Minor spec wording clarity needed in §1.3 `SyncValue` non-finite rejection and §1.5.1 baseline explanation, but code implements intent correctly.

## password_input drift findings

### Critical (spec mismatch)

**None.** Parts, State, Event, Context, Props, and machine logic all align with spec §1.1–1.5 and anatomy §2.

### Doc comment gaps

All public items have `///` documentation:
- `State` (enum) — documented ✓
- `Event` variants — all documented ✓
- `Context` fields — all documented ✓
- `Props` struct and builder methods — all documented ✓
- `Messages` struct — documented ✓
- `Machine` — documented ✓
- `Part` enum — all documented ✓
- `Api<'a>` and all methods — all documented ✓
- Helper functions (`props_output_changed`, `set_described_by`) — not public, no doc needed ✓

### Style/lint

1. **`api_error_message`** — Spec uses `AriaAttr::ErrorMessage` (lines 652–654 in impl), but also maintains `aria-describedby` for accessibility chain (line 781). Correct per spec §3.1.

2. **Key handling** — No `Key` type issues; all IDs and parts use `String` consistently ✓

3. **`no_cleanup()` vs `Box::new(|| {})`** — No explicit effect cleanup code needed; machine declares `type Effect = NoEffect` (line 321) ✓

4. **`#[expect]` vs `#[allow]`** — No lint suppressions in code ✓

5. **Disabled blocks visibility** — Lines 362–364 correctly prevent `ToggleVisibility` when disabled; lines 382–384 prevent `SetVisibility` when disabled. Spec silent on this, but matches Ark UI precedent ✓

6. **Root ID assignment** — Line 574 sets `HtmlAttr::Id` on root with `self.ctx.ids.id()`. Spec shows root as data-attrs only (line 295), but adding id is a safe enhancement ✓

7. **`aria-errorMessage` vs `aria-describedby`** — Implementation uses both (lines 652–654 set `aria-errorMessage` explicitly; line 781 merges into `aria-describedby`). Spec §3.1 lists only `aria-labelledby` and `aria-describedby`, not `aria-errorMessage`. This is a minor ARIA divergence (aria-errorMessage is deprecated in favor of describedby alone).

### Notes

- **Event::Change not in spec** — Implementation includes `Event::Change(String)` (lines 62, 418–434) to capture user input in uncontrolled mode. Spec does not list this event, but it's necessary for the uncontrolled pattern and is well-motivated in the comment (lines 57–61). Strong implementation addition.

- **Event::SetValue extended in spec** — Spec lists `SetValue(bool)` for controlled visibility (§1.2, line 44), but implementation interprets it as `SetValue(Option<String>)` for controlled *value*. This is a **major drift**: spec expects controlled visibility, implementation does controlled input value. Check spec §6.1 notes—it mentions "Controlled visibility: Ark UI supports visible: boolean...ars-ui currently uses default_visible". Implementation aligns with this noted design choice, not a bug.

- **`aria-errorMessage` attribute** — Lines 652–654 add `aria-errorMessage` when `invalid=true`. This is non-standard ARIA (deprecated/not in modern WAI-ARIA spec). Should use only `aria-describedby` per spec. **Minor doc/lint issue**.

- **Props::new() builder** — Implementation adds `Props::new()` (line 165) and builder methods (lines 171–288) not mentioned in spec. Good practice for ergonomics.

- **Diff from spec init logic** — Spec §1.5 shows `init` returning a tuple directly; implementation wraps in a tuple but with correct semantics ✓

---

**Summary**: Implementation is 99% spec-aligned. Single minor issue: `aria-errorMessage` attribute is non-standard; should rely solely on `aria-describedby`. No state machine, part, or event enum drift. Doc coverage is complete.

## search_input drift findings

### Critical (spec mismatch)

**Context field mismatch**: Spec §1.3 defines `Context` with `has_description`, but implementation adds `debounce_pending` (line 134) not in spec. Implementation also omits `messages: Messages` replication from spec (line 225-228 shows init but spec doesn't reference storing both locale and messages together in Context—this is an implementation detail not called out).

**Event::SetProps scope**: Spec §1.2 lists `SetProps` as syncing "output-affecting props," but implementation (line 557-562) syncs `disabled`, `readonly`, `invalid`, `placeholder`, `name` — missing `required` and `form` which appear in Props but not in transition. `required` is only read in `input_attrs()` (line 757), never synced to Context. `form` is only read from props in `input_attrs()` (line 777), never stored. **Spec and implementation both have this gap**: neither documents whether `required` or `form` changes propagate at runtime.

### Doc comment gaps

- `Effect::SearchDebounce` enum has doc comment (lines 342-350) ✓
- `Props::new()` (line 203-204) has `#[must_use]` but minimal doc
- Helper functions `props_output_changed()` (line 905) and `set_described_by()` (line 916) are private, so no pub doc required ✓
- All public `Api` methods have `#[must_use]` and doc comments ✓

**All `pub` items have `///` doc comments.** ✓

### Style/lint

- State enum uses `Eq` derive (line 25) matching spec Copy/Debug/PartialEq intent ✓
- Event enum uses `Eq` derive (line 48) instead of matching spec; spec shows `PartialEq` only. Implementation adds `Eq` which is more strict but fine (events are tiny).
- Props derives `HasId` (line 159) ✓, plus `Eq` instead of just spec's implied `PartialEq` — again stricter, fine.
- Context stores `locale` and `messages` (lines 149-152) — spec shows both in init and Messages example, implementation mirrors this correctly ✓
- No `#[expect]` or `#[allow]` lint suppressions found in production code ✓
- No TODO/FIXME in production code ✓

### Notes

1. **`debounce_pending` context field**: Implementation tracks whether a debounce timer is "currently pending" (line 126-133). Spec §1.5 implies this is implicit in the effect system but doesn't explicitly define this state field. No drift—just an internal optimization not called out in spec.

2. **Error message attribute**: Implementation adds `aria-errorMessage` (line 751-754) when invalid, not mentioned in spec §3.1. Spec only calls for `aria-describedby`. Adapter layer may rely on this; worth documenting.

3. **Readonly disables clear trigger** (line 807): Spec §1.7 and §3.2 don't explicitly forbid clear in readonly mode; implementation correctly blocks it. Comment on line 806 explains reasoning.

4. **Event enum completeness**: All spec events present (Focus, Blur, Change, Clear, Submit, SetSearching, DebounceExpired, CancelDebounce, RestartDebounce, CompositionStart, CompositionEnd, SetValue, SetProps, SetHasDescription) ✓

5. **Parts enum**: All spec parts present (Root, Label, Input, ClearTrigger, SubmitTrigger, LoadingIndicator, Description, ErrorMessage) ✓

6. **No hidden input**: Spec notes "no hidden input is emitted" (line 5 of implementation module doc)—correct per spec §5.

- Manually implements `PartialEq`, `Eq`, `Hash` with special handling for `Marker { value: f64 }` and `Thumb`/`HiddenInput { thumb }` variants to use `to_bits()` for NaN/float stability (lines 798–824), matching spec comment (line 376 of spec).
- `ComponentPart` impl correctly returns `"range-slider"` scope and static part names.

**Messages (line 371–389):**
- Derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq` but **NOT** `ComponentMessages` via derive macro — implements manually (line 389), consistent with spec.

**Machine impl (lines 414–742):**
- `init()` calls `normalize_values()` on both controlled and uncontrolled paths (lines 416–420).
- `transition()` correctly gates value-changing events on `disabled`/`readonly` (lines 467–480).
- `SetProps` updates `min`, `max`, `step`, `large_step`, `min_steps_between`, `allow_thumb_swap`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `invalid`, `orientation`, `dir`, `thumb_alignment`, `name`, `form`, `start_disabled`, `end_disabled` and resnaps active drag bookkeeping (lines 644–692), matching spec §1.7.
- `PointerUp` uses `drag_value` for controlled commits (line 566), matching spec §1.7 ("using `drag_value` so controlled drags commit the pending value").

**Effect closures (lines 1470–1494):**
- Both effects invoke callbacks inside closures with captured `value` — correctly structured.

### Notes
- Range slider passes comprehensive test suite (3396 lines total; 2756–3395 are tests).
- Helper functions (`normalize_values`, `snap_to_step`, `effective_gap`, etc.) cover all spec guards (§1.5).
- RTL and vertical orientation fully tested.
- Drag-past swap behavior tested (clamp mode default, swap when `allow_thumb_swap: true`).
- All spec-defined parts render with correct ARIA, data attributes, and styles.
- Value formatter receives `(this_value, other_value)` tuple per spec §2 (line 1092).

**No drift detected.**

Perfect. Now let me create a comprehensive audit report. Let me summarize the findings:

## radio_group drift findings

### Critical (spec mismatch)

**1. ErrorMessage aria-live attribute mismatch (line 890)**

Spec (§1.7 API, line 564): `attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), "polite");`

Implementation (line 890): `attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "alert");`

The spec defines error_message_attrs to set `aria-live="polite"`, but implementation sets `role="alert"` instead. Per WCAG, `role="alert"` is semantically stronger than `aria-live="polite"` and conflicts with spec design intent. This must be reconciled—either align implementation to spec or update spec to document the alert role as intentional enhancement.

**2. on_item_control_keydown keyboard behavior enhancement (lines 1070–1108)**

Implementation adds:
- `!data.repeat` guard on Space/Enter (prevents auto-repeat activation)
- `!self.is_item_disabled(item_value)` guard (prevents disabled item activation)
- Explicit RTL + Horizontal orientation checks for arrow keys

Spec (line 699–710) shows simpler logic without repeat/disabled guards or directional checks. Implementation is more robust but diverges from spec. Update spec to document these guards, or align implementation to spec.

### Doc comment gaps

All public types (State, Event, Context, Radio, Props, Messages, Effect, Machine, Part, Api) and their fields have doc comments. **No gaps found.**

### Style/lint

None detected:
- No `#[expect]` or `#[allow]` without justification
- Uses `no_cleanup()` correctly (line 1255)
- No TODO/FIXME/deprecated markers
- No loose `Box::new(|| {})` patterns
- All identifiers use `Key` enum (not raw strings)

### Notes

- Implementation is significantly more defensive than spec (disabled-item checks, repeat guards, orientation-aware RTL handling in on_item_control_keydown)
- Spec lacks detail on error message ARIA semantics (aria-live vs role="alert")
- Excellent test coverage (60+ tests) validates both happy and edge paths
- Consider updating spec §1.7 to document: (a) error_message_attrs role intent, (b) on_item_control_keydown guards for repeat/disabled/RTL
```

1	//! Spec-conformance tests for ars-components.
2	//!
3	//! Each module asserts that a component's `Part` enum matches the
4	//! anatomy table declared in `spec/components/.../{component}.md` §3.
5	//! The test layer is intentionally tiny — it leans on the derive-generated
6	//! `Part::scope()`, `Part::all()`, and `Part::name()` to compare against a
7	//! hand-rolled allow-list of `(Part, expected-kebab-name)` pairs per
8	//! component.
9	//!
10	//! New components SHOULD add a sibling module here (or extend an existing
11	//! one) so any drift between the spec's declared parts and the impl is
12	//! caught at build time, not by a reviewer's eye.

25	    let mut service = Service::<dialog::Machine>::new(
26	        dialog::Props::new()
27	            .id("dialog")
28	            .default_open(true)
29	            .role(dialog::Role::AlertDialog),
30	        &Env::default(),
31	        &dialog::Messages::default(),
32	    );
33	
34	    drop(service.send(dialog::Event::RegisterTitle));
35	    drop(service.send(dialog::Event::RegisterDescription));
36	
37	    let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).content_attrs();
38	
39	    assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("alertdialog"));
40	    assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Modal)), Some("true"));
41	    assert_eq!(
42	        attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy)),
43	        Some("dialog-title")
44	    );
45	    assert_eq!(
46	        attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),
47	        Some("dialog-description")
48	    );
49	}

410	    #[test]
411	    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
412	    fn proptest_switch_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(
413	        props in arb_switch_props(),
414	        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_switch_event(), 0..128),
415	    ) {
416	        let mut service = Service::<switch::Machine>::new(
417	            props,
418	            &Env::default(),
419	            &switch::Messages,
420	        );
421	
422	        for event in events {
423	            drop(service.send(event));
424	
425	            let state = *service.state();
426	
427	            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), "switch");
428	            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().checked.get(), &switch_state_checked(state));
429	
430	            let control_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).control_attrs();
431	
432	            prop_assert_eq!(control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("switch"));
433	            prop_assert_eq!(
434	                control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Checked)),
435	                Some(switch_aria_checked_token(state))
436	            );
437	
438	            let hidden_input_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).hidden_input_attrs();
439	
440	            if service.context().disabled {
441	                prop_assert!(hidden_input_attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));
442	            }
443	
444	            if service.context().required {
445	                prop_assert!(hidden_input_attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required));
446	            }
447	
448	            let described_by = match (service.context().has_description, service.context().invalid) {
449	                (false, false) => None,
450	                (true, false) => Some("switch-description"),
451	                (false, true) => Some("switch-error-message"),
452	                (true, true) => Some("switch-description switch-error-message"),
453	            };
454	
455	            prop_assert_eq!(
456	                control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),
457	                described_by,
458	            );
459	        }
460	    }

462	    #[test]
463	    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
464	    fn proptest_editable_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(
465	        props in arb_editable_props(),
466	        actions in prop::collection::vec(arb_editable_action(), 0..128),
467	    ) {
468	        let mut service = Service::<editable::Machine>::new(
469	            props.clone(),
470	            &Env::default(),
471	            &editable::Messages::default(),
472	        );
473	
474	        let mut expected_committed = props.default_value.clone();
475	        let mut expected_edit = props.default_value.clone();
476	        let mut expected_state = editable::State::Preview;
477	        let mut expected_composing = false;
478	
479	        for action in actions {
480	            let event = match action {
481	                EditableAction::Activate => {
482	                    if !props.disabled && !props.readonly && expected_state == editable::State::Preview {
483	                        expected_state = editable::State::Editing;
484	                        expected_edit = expected_committed.clone();
485	                    }
486	
487	                    editable::Event::Activate
488	                }
489	
490	                EditableAction::Change(value) => {
491	                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing
492	                        && !props.disabled
493	                        && !props.readonly
494	                        && !expected_composing
495	                    {
496	                        expected_edit = clamp_editable_text(&value);
497	                    }
498	
499	                    editable::Event::Change(value)
500	                }
501	
502	                EditableAction::Submit(value) => {
503	                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing
504	                        && !props.disabled
505	                        && !props.readonly
506	                        && !expected_composing
507	                    {
508	                        let value = clamp_editable_text(&value);
509	
510	                        expected_committed = value.clone();
511	                        expected_edit = value;
512	                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;
513	                        expected_composing = false;
514	                    }
515	
516	                    editable::Event::Submit(value)
517	                }
518	
519	                EditableAction::Cancel => {
520	                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing {
521	                        expected_edit = expected_committed.clone();
522	                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;
523	                        expected_composing = false;
524	                    }
525	
526	                    editable::Event::Cancel
527	                }
528	
529	                EditableAction::Focus(is_keyboard) => editable::Event::Focus { is_keyboard },
530	
531	                EditableAction::Blur => {
532	                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing && !expected_composing {
533	                        expected_committed = expected_edit.clone();
534	                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;
535	                        expected_composing = false;
536	                    }
537	
538	                    editable::Event::Blur
539	                }
540	
541	                EditableAction::CompositionStart => {
542	                    expected_composing = true;
543	
544	                    editable::Event::CompositionStart
545	                }
546	
547	                EditableAction::CompositionEnd(value) => {
548	                    expected_composing = false;
549	
550	                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing && !props.disabled && !props.readonly {
551	                        expected_edit = clamp_editable_text(&value);
552	                    }
553	
554	                    editable::Event::CompositionEnd(value)
555	                }
556	            };
557	
558	            drop(service.send(event));
559	
560	            let ctx = service.context();
561	
562	            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();
563	
564	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), "editable");
565	            prop_assert_eq!(*service.state(), expected_state);
566	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get(), &expected_committed);
567	            prop_assert_eq!(&ctx.edit_value, &expected_edit);
568	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.is_composing, expected_composing);
569	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.edit_value.as_str()));
570	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);
571	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);
572	
573	            if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {
574	                prop_assert_ne!(*service.state(), editable::State::Editing);
575	            }
576	        }
577	    }
578	
579	    #[test]
580	    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
581	    fn proptest_text_field_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(
582	        props in arb_text_field_props(),
583	        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_text_field_event(), 0..128),
584	    ) {
585	        let mut service = Service::<text_field::Machine>::new(
586	            props,
587	            &Env::default(),
588	            &text_field::Messages::default(),
589	        );
590	
591	        for event in events {
592	            drop(service.send(event));
593	
594	            let ctx = service.context();
595	
596	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), "text-field");
597	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused, service.state() == &text_field::State::Focused);
598	
599	            if !ctx.focused {
600	                prop_assert!(!ctx.focus_visible);
601	            }
602	
603	            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();
604	
605	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.value.get().as_str()));
606	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);
607	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);
608	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required), ctx.required);
609	
610	            if ctx.invalid {
611	                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some("true"));
612	            } else {
613	                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), None);
614	            }
615	
616	            let described_by = match (ctx.has_description, ctx.invalid) {
617	                (false, false) => None,
618	                (true, false) => Some("text-field-description"),
619	                (false, true) => Some("text-field-error-message"),
620	                (true, true) => Some("text-field-description text-field-error-message"),
621	            };
622	
623	            prop_assert_eq!(
624	                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),
625	                described_by,
626	            );
627	        }
628	    }

630	    #[test]
631	    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
632	    fn proptest_textarea_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(
633	        props in arb_textarea_props(),
634	        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_textarea_event(), 0..128),
635	    ) {
636	        let mut service = Service::<textarea::Machine>::new(
637	            props,
638	            &Env::default(),
639	            &textarea::Messages,
640	        );
641	
642	        for event in events {
643	            let result = service.send(event);
644	
645	            let ctx = service.context();
646	
647	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), "textarea");
648	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused, service.state() == &textarea::State::Focused);
649	
650	            if !ctx.focused {
651	                prop_assert!(!ctx.focus_visible);
652	            }
653	
654	            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).textarea_attrs();
655	
656	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.value.get().as_str()));
657	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);
658	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);
659	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required), ctx.required);
660	
661	            let rows = ctx.rows.to_string();
662	
663	            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Rows), Some(rows.as_str()));
664	
665	            if ctx.invalid {
666	                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some("true"));
667	            } else {
668	                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), None);
669	            }
670	
671	            let described_by = match (ctx.has_description, ctx.invalid) {
672	                (false, false) => None,
673	                (true, false) => Some("textarea-description"),
674	                (false, true) => Some("textarea-error-message"),
675	                (true, true) => Some("textarea-description textarea-error-message"),
676	            };
677	
678	            prop_assert_eq!(
679	                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),
680	                described_by,
681	            );
682	
683	            for effect in &result.pending_effects {
684	                if effect.name == textarea::Effect::AutoResize {
685	                    prop_assert_eq!(
686	                        effect.metadata.as_ref(),
687	                        Some(&EffectMetadata::ResizeToContent(ars_core::ResizeToContentEffect {
688	                            element_id: "textarea-textarea".to_string(),
689	                            max_height: ctx.max_height.clone(),
690	                            max_rows: ctx.max_rows,
691	                        })),
692	                    );
693	                }
694	            }
695	        }
696	    }
697	}

> AGENT

The convention is clear: per-component files with shared helpers in `mod.rs`. I'll use direct per-submodule component imports (cleanest, no collision with module names) and hoist the two genuinely-shared strategies (`arb_short_text`, `arb_slider_step`) into `mod.rs`. Writing the 11 existing-proptest files plus `mod.rs` now.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/audit-input-components/crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/input/mod.rs",
  "content": "//! Ignored nightly property-based tests for `crates/ars-components/src/input/*`.\n//!\n//! Mirrors the per-component layout used by `overlay/` and `utility/`: each\n//! component owns a sibling module holding its arbitraries and `proptest!`\n//! block. Strategies shared by more than one component live here so they are\n//! defined once.\n\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nmod checkbox;\nmod checkbox_group;\nmod editable;\nmod number_input;\nmod password_input;\nmod pin_input;\nmod radio_group;\nmod range_slider;\nmod search_input;\nmod slider;\nmod switch;\nmod text_field;\nmod textarea;\n// `file_trigger` is a stateless component (no `Machine`/`State`/`Event`), so it\n// has no state-machine invariants to property-test. Its connect-API contract is\n// covered by the unit and spec-conformance suites instead.\n\n/// Short lowercase strings used as text-input values across the text-bearing\n/// input components (text-field, textarea, editable, password, search).\nfn arb_short_text() -> impl Strategy<Value = String> {\n    \"[a-z]{0,16}\".prop_map(String::from)\n}\n\n/// Discrete slider steps shared by the single-thumb and range sliders.\nfn arb_slider_step() -> impl Strategy<Value = f64> {\n    prop_oneof![Just(1.0), Just(2.0), Just(5.0), Just(10.0)]\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::checkbox;\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nfn arb_checkbox_state() -> impl Strategy<Value = checkbox::State> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(checkbox::State::Unchecked),\n        Just(checkbox::State::Checked),\n        Just(checkbox::State::Indeterminate),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn arb_checkbox_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = checkbox::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_checkbox_state()),\n        arb_checkbox_state(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        prop::option::of(\"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from)),\n        prop::option::of(\"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from)),\n        \"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                checked,\n                default_checked,\n                disabled,\n                required,\n                invalid,\n                readonly,\n                name,\n                form,\n                value,\n            )| {\n                checkbox::Props {\n                    id: \"checkbox\".to_string(),\n                    checked,\n                    default_checked,\n                    disabled,\n                    required,\n                    invalid,\n                    readonly,\n                    name,\n                    form,\n                    value,\n                    on_checked_change: None,\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_checkbox_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = checkbox::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(checkbox::Event::Toggle),\n        Just(checkbox::Event::Check),\n        Just(checkbox::Event::Uncheck),\n        Just(checkbox::Event::Reset),\n        prop::option::of(arb_checkbox_state()).prop_map(checkbox::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(checkbox::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(checkbox::Event::SetHasDescription),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| checkbox::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(checkbox::Event::Blur),\n    ]\n}\n\nconst fn aria_checked_token(state: checkbox::State) -> &'static str {\n    match state {\n        checkbox::State::Unchecked => \"false\",\n        checkbox::State::Checked => \"true\",\n        checkbox::State::Indeterminate => \"mixed\",\n    }\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_checkbox_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_checkbox_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_checkbox_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<checkbox::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &checkbox::Messages,\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let state = *service.state();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), \"checkbox\");\n            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().checked.get(), &state);\n\n            let control_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).control_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Checked)),\n                Some(aria_checked_token(state))\n            );\n\n            let hidden_input_attrs = service.connect(&|_|   "content": "use ars_components::input::switch;\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Direction, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nfn arb_switch_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = switch::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(any::<bool>()),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        prop::option::of(\"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from)),\n        prop::option::of(\"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from)),\n        \"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from),\n        prop::option::of(\"[a-z]{1,8}\".prop_map(String::from)),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                checked,\n                default_checked,\n                disabled,\n                required,\n                invalid,\n                readonly,\n                name,\n                form,\n                value,\n                label,\n                rtl,\n            )| switch::Props {\n                id: \"switch\".to_string(),\n                checked,\n                default_checked,\n                disabled,\n                required,\n                invalid,\n                readonly,\n                name,\n                form,\n                value,\n                label,\n                dir: if rtl { Direction::Rtl } else { Direction::Ltr },\n                on_checked_change: None,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_switch_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = switch::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(switch::Event::Toggle),\n        Just(switch::Event::TurnOn),\n        Just(switch::Event::TurnOff),\n        Just(switch::Event::Reset),\n        prop::option::of(any::<bool>()).prop_map(switch::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(switch::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(switch::Event::SetHasDescription),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| switch::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(switch::Event::Blur),\n    ]\n}\n\nconst fn switch_state_checked(state: switch::State) -> bool {\n    match state {\n        switch::State::Off => false,\n        switch::State::On => true,\n    }\n}\n\nconst fn switch_aria_checked_token(state: switch::State) -> &'static str {\n    match state {\n        switch::State::Off => \"false\",\n        switch::State::On => \"true\",\n    }\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_switch_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_switch_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_switch_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<switch::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &switch::Messages,\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let state = *service.state();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), \"switch\");\n            prop_assert_eq!(service.context().checked.get(), &switch_state_checked(state));\n\n            let control_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).control_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"switch\"));\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Checked)),\n                Some(switch_aria_checked_token(state))\n            );\n\n            let hidden_input_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).hidden_input_attrs();\n\n            if service.context().disabled {\n                prop_assert!(hidden_input_attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n            }\n\n            if service.context().required {\n                prop_assert!(hidden_input_attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required));\n            }\n\n            let described_by = match (service.context().has_description, service.context().invalid) {\n                (false, false) => None,\n                (true, false) => Some(\"switch-description\"),\n                (false, true) => Some(\"switch-error-message\"),\n                (true, true) => Some(\"switch-description switch-error-message\"),\n            };\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                control_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),\n                described_by,\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::text_field;\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Direction, Env, HtmlAttr, InputMode, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\nfn arb_text_field_input_type() -> impl Strategy<Value = text_field::InputType> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Text),\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Password),\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Email),\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Url),\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Tel),\n        Just(text_field::InputType::Search),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn arb_text_field_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = text_field::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()),\n        arb_short_text(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        arb_text_field_input_type(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                input_type,\n                clearable,\n                input_mode,\n            )| text_field::Props {\n                id: \"text-field\".to_string(),\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                placeholder: Some(\"placeholder\".to_string()),\n                input_type,\n                max_length: Some(64),\n                min_length: Some(1),\n                pattern: Some(\"[a-z]+\".to_string()),\n                autocomplete: Some(\"name\".to_string()),\n                name: Some(\"name\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                clearable,\n                dir: Direction::Ltr,\n                input_mode: input_mode.then_some(InputMode::Text),\n                on_focus_change: None,\n                on_value_change: None,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_text_field_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = text_field::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| text_field::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(text_field::Event::Blur),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(text_field::Event::Change),\n        Just(text_field::Event::Clear),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(text_field::Event::SetInvalid),\n        Just(text_field::Event::CompositionStart),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(text_field::Event::CompositionEnd),\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()).prop_map(text_field::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(text_field::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(text_field::Event::SetHasDescription),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_text_field_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_text_field_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_text_field_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<text_field::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &text_field::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), \"text-field\");\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused, service.state() == &text_field::State::Focused);\n\n            if !ctx.focused {\n                prop_assert!(!ctx.focus_visible);\n            }\n\n            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.value.get().as_str()));\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required), ctx.required);\n\n            if ctx.invalid {\n                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some(\"true\"));\n            } else {\n                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), None);\n            }\n\n            let described_by = match (ctx.has_description, ctx.invalid) {\n                (false, false) => None,\n                (true, false) => Some(\"text-field-description\"),\n                (false, true) => Some(\"text-field-error-message\"),\n                (true, true) => Some(\"text-field-description text-field-error-message\"),\n            };\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),\n                described_by,\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
