  "content": "use ars_components::input::editable;\nuse ars_core::{Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug)]\nenum EditableAction {\n    Activate,\n    Change(String),\n    Submit(String),\n    Cancel,\n    Focus(bool),\n    Blur,\n    CompositionStart,\n    CompositionEnd(String),\n}\n\nfn arb_editable_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = editable::Props> {\n    (\n        arb_short_text(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(default_value, disabled, readonly, invalid, required)| editable::Props {\n                id: \"editable\".to_string(),\n                value: None,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                submit_mode: editable::SubmitMode::Both,\n                activate_mode: editable::ActivateMode::DblClick,\n                auto_select: true,\n                placeholder: Some(\"placeholder\".to_string()),\n                max_length: Some(8),\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                name: Some(\"name\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                submit_on_blur: true,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_editable_action() -> impl Strategy<Value = EditableAction> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(EditableAction::Activate),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(EditableAction::Change),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(EditableAction::Submit),\n        Just(EditableAction::Cancel),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(EditableAction::Focus),\n        Just(EditableAction::Blur),\n        Just(EditableAction::CompositionStart),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(EditableAction::CompositionEnd),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn clamp_editable_text(value: &str) -> String {\n    value.chars().take(8).collect()\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_editable_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_editable_props(),\n        actions in prop::collection::vec(arb_editable_action(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<editable::Machine>::new(\n            props.clone(),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &editable::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        let mut expected_committed = props.default_value.clone();\n        let mut expected_edit = props.default_value.clone();\n        let mut expected_state = editable::State::Preview;\n        let mut expected_composing = false;\n\n        for action in actions {\n            let event = match action {\n                EditableAction::Activate => {\n                    if !props.disabled && !props.readonly && expected_state == editable::State::Preview {\n                        expected_state = editable::State::Editing;\n                        expected_edit = expected_committed.clone();\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::Activate\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::Change(value) => {\n                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing\n                        && !props.disabled\n                        && !props.readonly\n                        && !expected_composing\n                    {\n                        expected_edit = clamp_editable_text(&value);\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::Change(value)\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::Submit(value) => {\n                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing\n                        && !props.disabled\n                        && !props.readonly\n                        && !expected_composing\n                    {\n                        let value = clamp_editable_text(&value);\n\n                        expected_committed = value.clone();\n                        expected_edit = value;\n                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;\n                        expected_composing = false;\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::Submit(value)\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::Cancel => {\n                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing {\n                        expected_edit = expected_committed.clone();\n                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;\n                        expected_composing = false;\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::Cancel\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::Focus(is_keyboard) => editable::Event::Focus { is_keyboard },\n\n                EditableAction::Blur => {\n                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing && !expected_composing {\n                        expected_committed = expected_edit.clone();\n                        expected_state = editable::State::Preview;\n                        expected_composing = false;\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::Blur\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::CompositionStart => {\n                    expected_composing = true;\n\n                    editable::Event::CompositionStart\n                }\n\n                EditableAction::CompositionEnd(value) => {\n                    expected_composing = false;\n\n                    if expected_state == editable::State::Editing && !props.disabled && !props.readonly {\n                        expected_edit = clamp_editable_text(&value);\n                    }\n\n                    editable::Event::CompositionEnd(value)\n                }\n            };\n\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), \"editable\");\n            prop_assert_eq!(*service.state(), expected_state);\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get(), &expected_committed);\n            prop_assert_eq!(&ctx.edit_value, &expected_edit);\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.is_composing, expected_composing);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.edit_value.as_str()));\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);\n\n            if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {\n                prop_assert_ne!(*service.state(), editable::State::Editing);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::textarea;\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Direction, EffectMetadata, Env, HtmlAttr, InputMode, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\nfn arb_textarea_resize() -> impl Strategy<Value = textarea::ResizeMode> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(textarea::ResizeMode::None),\n        Just(textarea::ResizeMode::Both),\n        Just(textarea::ResizeMode::Horizontal),\n        Just(textarea::ResizeMode::Vertical),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn arb_textarea_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = textarea::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()),\n        arb_short_text(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        1_u32..12,\n        prop::option::of(1_u32..80),\n        arb_textarea_resize(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                rows,\n                cols,\n                resize,\n                auto_resize,\n                input_mode,\n            )| textarea::Props {\n                id: \"textarea\".to_string(),\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                placeholder: Some(\"placeholder\".to_string()),\n                max_length: Some(256),\n                min_length: Some(1),\n                name: Some(\"bio\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                autocomplete: Some(\"off\".to_string()),\n                rows,\n                cols,\n                resize,\n                auto_resize,\n                max_height: Some(\"240px\".to_string()),\n                max_rows: Some(8),\n                dir: Direction::Ltr,\n                input_mode: input_mode.then_some(InputMode::Text),\n                on_value_change: None,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_textarea_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = textarea::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| textarea::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(textarea::Event::Blur),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(textarea::Event::Change),\n        Just(textarea::Event::Clear),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(textarea::Event::SetInvalid),\n        Just(textarea::Event::CompositionStart),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(textarea::Event::CompositionEnd),\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()).prop_map(textarea::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(textarea::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(textarea::Event::SetHasDescription),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_textarea_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_textarea_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_textarea_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<textarea::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &textarea::Messages,\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            let result = service.send(event);\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.ids.id(), \"textarea\");\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused, service.state() == &textarea::State::Focused);\n\n            if !ctx.focused {\n                prop_assert!(!ctx.focus_visible);\n            }\n\n            let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).textarea_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value), Some(ctx.value.get().as_str()));\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled), ctx.disabled);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::ReadOnly), ctx.readonly);\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Required), ctx.required);\n\n            let rows = ctx.rows.to_string();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Rows), Some(rows.as_str()));\n\n            if ctx.invalid {\n                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), Some(\"true\"));\n            } else {\n                prop_assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Invalid)), None);\n            }\n\n            let described_by = match (ctx.has_description, ctx.invalid) {\n                (false, false) => None,\n                (true, false) => Some(\"textarea-description\"),\n                (false, true) => Some(\"textarea-error-message\"),\n                (true, true) => Some(\"textarea-description textarea-error-message\"),\n            };\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::DescribedBy)),\n                described_by,\n            );\n\n            for effect in &result.pending_effects {\n                if effect.name == textarea::Effect::AutoResize {\n                    prop_assert_eq!(\n                        effect.metadata.as_ref(),\n                        Some(&EffectMetadata::ResizeToContent(ars_core::ResizeToContentEffect {\n                            element_id: \"textarea-textarea\".to_string(),\n                            max_height: ctx.max_height.clone(),\n                            max_rows: ctx.max_rows,\n                        })),\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::password_input;\nuse ars_core::{Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\nfn arb_password_input_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = password_input::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()),\n        arb_short_text(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(value, default_value, disabled, required, invalid, readonly, default_visible)| {\n                password_input::Props {\n                    id: \"password-input\".to_string(),\n                    value,\n                    default_value,\n                    disabled,\n                    required,\n                    invalid,\n                    readonly,\n                    default_visible,\n                    placeholder: Some(\"Password\".to_string()),\n                    name: Some(\"password\".to_string()),\n                    form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                    autocomplete: Some(\"current-password\".to_string()),\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_password_input_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = password_input::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(password_input::Event::ToggleVisibility),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(password_input::Event::SetVisibility),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| password_input::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(password_input::Event::Blur),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_password_input_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_password_input_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_password_input_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<password_input::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &password_input::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n            let state = service.state();\n\n            // visibility flag tracks state\n            match state {\n                password_input::State::Masked => prop_assert!(!ctx.visible),\n                password_input::State::Visible => prop_assert!(ctx.visible),\n            }\n\n            let input_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();\n\n            let ty = if ctx.visible { \"text\" } else { \"password\" };\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(input_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(ty));\n\n            if ctx.disabled {\n                prop_assert!(input_attrs.contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::search_input;\nuse ars_core::{Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\nfn arb_search_input_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = search_input::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_short_text()),\n        arb_short_text(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        prop::option::of(1_u64..=500),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(value, default_value, disabled, readonly, invalid, required, debounce_ms)| {\n                search_input::Props {\n                    id: \"search-input\".to_string(),\n                    value,\n                    default_value,\n                    disabled,\n                    readonly,\n                    invalid,\n                    required,\n                    placeholder: Some(\"Search...\".to_string()),\n                    name: Some(\"q\".to_string()),\n                    form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                    debounce: debounce_ms.map(core::time::Duration::from_millis),\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_search_input_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = search_input::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| search_input::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(search_input::Event::Blur),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(search_input::Event::Change),\n        Just(search_input::Event::Clear),\n        Just(search_input::Event::Submit),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(search_input::Event::SetSearching),\n        Just(search_input::Event::DebounceExpired),\n        Just(search_input::Event::CancelDebounce),\n        Just(search_input::Event::CompositionStart),\n        Just(search_input::Event::CompositionEnd),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_search_input_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_search_input_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_search_input_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<search_input::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &search_input::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            let result = service.send(event);\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n            let state = *service.state();\n\n            // Loading flag aligns with Searching state on entry, but Idle/Focused\n            // can be returned to while loading=false. Just check the converse:\n            // when state == Searching, loading must be true (set by Submit/SetSearching).\n            if state == search_input::State::Searching {\n                prop_assert!(ctx.loading);\n            }\n\n            // is_composing never co-occurs with a fresh debounce effect\n            if ctx.is_composing {\n                for effect in &result.pending_effects {\n                    prop_assert!(effect.name != search_input::Effect::SearchDebounce);\n                }\n            }\n\n            let input_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(input_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(\"search\"));\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::number_input;\nuse ars_core::{Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_short_text;\n\nfn arb_number_input_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = number_input::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(-1000.0_f64..1000.0),\n        prop::option::of(-1000.0_f64..1000.0),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        prop::option::of(0_u32..=4),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                allow_mouse_wheel,\n                spin_on_press,\n                precision,\n            )| number_input::Props {\n                id: \"number-input\".to_string(),\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                min: -1000.0,\n                max: 1000.0,\n                step: 1.0,\n                large_step: 10.0,\n                precision,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                required,\n                name: Some(\"qty\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                allow_mouse_wheel,\n                clamp_value_on_blur: true,\n                spin_on_press,\n                format_options: None,\n                display_format: None,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_number_input_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = number_input::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| number_input::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(number_input::Event::Blur),\n        arb_short_text().prop_map(number_input::Event::Change),\n        Just(number_input::Event::Increment),\n        Just(number_input::Event::Decrement),\n        Just(number_input::Event::IncrementLarge),\n        Just(number_input::Event::DecrementLarge),\n        Just(number_input::Event::IncrementToMax),\n        Just(number_input::Event::DecrementToMin),\n        (-1000.0_f64..1000.0).prop_map(number_input::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(number_input::Event::StartScrub),\n        (-10.0_f64..10.0).prop_map(number_input::Event::Scrub),\n        Just(number_input::Event::EndScrub),\n        (-5.0_f64..5.0).prop_map(|delta| number_input::Event::Wheel { delta }),\n        Just(number_input::Event::CompositionStart),\n        Just(number_input::Event::CompositionEnd),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_number_input_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_number_input_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_number_input_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<number_input::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &number_input::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            // value is always inside [min, max] when Some and the machine has not\n            // observed a `Change` since the last clamp opportunity (Blur with\n            // clamp_value_on_blur). Increment/Decrement/SetValue all clamp.\n            // Change(text) does NOT clamp, so we only assert the clamping\n            // happens for non-Change-only paths by checking after a Blur.\n            if let Some(value) = ctx.value.get() {\n                prop_assert!(!value.is_nan(), \"value never becomes NaN\");\n            }\n\n            let input_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).input_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(input_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"spinbutton\"));\n            prop_assert_eq!(input_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::InputMode), Some(\"decimal\"));\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::slider;\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Direction, Env, HtmlAttr, Orientation, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_slider_step;\n\nfn arb_slider_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = slider::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(0.0_f64..100.0),\n        0.0_f64..100.0,\n        arb_slider_step(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(value, default_value, step, disabled, readonly, invalid, vertical, rtl, discrete)| {\n                slider::Props {\n                    id: \"slider\".to_string(),\n                    value,\n                    default_value,\n                    min: 0.0,\n                    max: 100.0,\n                    step,\n                    large_step: Some(step * 10.0),\n                    disabled,\n                    readonly,\n                    invalid,\n                    orientation: if vertical {\n                        Orientation::Vertical\n                    } else {\n                        Orientation::Horizontal\n                    },\n                    dir: if rtl { Direction::Rtl } else { Direction::Ltr },\n                    origin: slider::Origin::Start,\n                    name: Some(\"volume\".to_string()),\n                    form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                    marks: Vec::new(),\n                    tick_format: None,\n                    value_format: None,\n                    format_value: None,\n                    format_value_text: None,\n                    discrete,\n                    value_labels: discrete\n                        .then(|| vec![\"Low\".to_string(), \"Medium\".to_string(), \"High\".to_string()]),\n                    thumb_alignment: slider::ThumbAlignment::Contain,\n                    on_value_change: None,\n                    on_value_change_end: None,\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_slider_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = slider::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| slider::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(slider::Event::Blur),\n        (0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(|value| slider::Event::PointerDown { value }),\n        (0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(|value| slider::Event::PointerMove { value }),\n        Just(slider::Event::PointerUp),\n        Just(slider::Event::Increment),\n        Just(slider::Event::Decrement),\n        Just(slider::Event::IncrementLarge),\n        Just(slider::Event::DecrementLarge),\n        Just(slider::Event::SetToMin),\n        Just(slider::Event::SetToMax),\n        (0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(slider::Event::SetValue),\n        prop::option::of(0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(slider::Event::SyncValue),\n        Just(slider::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(slider::Event::SetHasDescription),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_slider_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_slider_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_slider_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<slider::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &slider::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            let previous = *service.context().value.get();\n\n            let interactive_mutation = matches!(\n                event,\n                slider::Event::PointerDown { .. }\n                    | slider::Event::PointerMove { .. }\n                    | slider::Event::Increment\n                    | slider::Event::Decrement\n                    | slider::Event::IncrementLarge\n                    | slider::Event::DecrementLarge\n                    | slider::Event::SetToMin\n                    | slider::Event::SetToMax\n                    | slider::Event::SetValue(_)\n            );\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n            let value = *ctx.value.get();\n\n            prop_assert!(value.is_finite());\n            prop_assert!(value >= ctx.min - f64::EPSILON);\n            prop_assert!(value <= ctx.max + f64::EPSILON);\n\n            let steps = (value - ctx.min) / ctx.step;\n\n            prop_assert!(\n                (steps - steps.round()).abs() <= 1.0e-9,\n                \"value {value} must stay on step grid {}..{} step {}\",\n                ctx.min,\n                ctx.max,\n                ctx.step\n            );\n\n            if (ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly) && interactive_mutation {\n                prop_assert_eq!(value, previous);\n            }\n\n            let thumb_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).thumb_attrs();\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(thumb_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"slider\"));\n\n            let aria_value = thumb_attrs\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow))\n                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<f64>().ok())\n                .expect(\"aria-valuenow must parse\");\n\n            prop_assert!(aria_value >= ctx.min - f64::EPSILON);\n            prop_assert!(aria_value <= ctx.max + f64::EPSILON);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::{range_slider, slider};\nuse ars_core::{AriaAttr, Direction, Env, HtmlAttr, Orientation, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nuse super::arb_slider_step;\n\nfn arb_range_slider_values() -> impl Strategy<Value = [f64; 2]> {\n    (0.0_f64..100.0, 0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(|(start, end)| [start, end])\n}\n\nfn arb_range_slider_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = range_slider::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_range_slider_values()),\n        arb_range_slider_values(),\n        arb_slider_step(),\n        0_u32..=4,\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                step,\n                min_steps_between,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                vertical,\n                rtl,\n                allow_thumb_swap,\n                start_disabled,\n                end_disabled,\n            )| range_slider::Props {\n                id: \"range-slider\".to_string(),\n                value,\n                default_value,\n                min: 0.0,\n                max: 100.0,\n                step,\n                large_step: Some(step * 10.0),\n                min_steps_between,\n                disabled,\n                readonly,\n                invalid,\n                orientation: if vertical {\n                    Orientation::Vertical\n                } else {\n                    Orientation::Horizontal\n                },\n                dir: if rtl { Direction::Rtl } else { Direction::Ltr },\n                name: Some(\"price\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                allow_thumb_swap,\n                start_disabled,\n                end_disabled,\n                format_value: None,\n                thumb_alignment: slider::ThumbAlignment::Contain,\n                on_value_change: None,\n                on_value_change_end: None,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_range_slider_thumb() -> impl Strategy<Value = range_slider::ThumbIndex> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(range_slider::ThumbIndex::Start),\n        Just(range_slider::ThumbIndex::End),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn arb_range_slider_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = range_slider::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        (arb_range_slider_thumb(), any::<bool>())\n            .prop_map(|(thumb, is_keyboard)| { range_slider::Event::Focus { thumb, is_keyboard } }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::Blur { thumb }),\n        (arb_range_slider_thumb(), 0.0_f64..100.0)\n            .prop_map(|(thumb, value)| { range_slider::Event::PointerDown { thumb, value } }),\n        (0.0_f64..100.0).prop_map(|value| range_slider::Event::PointerMove { value }),\n        Just(range_slider::Event::PointerUp),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::Increment { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::Decrement { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::IncrementLarge { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::DecrementLarge { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::SetToMin { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_thumb().prop_map(|thumb| range_slider::Event::SetToMax { thumb }),\n        arb_range_slider_values().prop_map(range_slider::Event::SetValues),\n        prop::option::of(arb_range_slider_values()).prop_map(range_slider::Event::SyncValue),\n        Just(range_slider::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(range_slider::Event::SetHasDescription),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(range_slider::Event::SetHasLabel),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_range_slider_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_range_slider_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_range_slider_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<range_slider::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &range_slider::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            let previous = *service.context().value.get();\n\n            let interactive_mutation = matches!(\n                event,\n                range_slider::Event::PointerDown { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::PointerMove { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::Increment { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::Decrement { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::IncrementLarge { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::DecrementLarge { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::SetToMin { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::SetToMax { .. }\n                    | range_slider::Event::SetValues(_)\n            );\n\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            let value = *ctx.value.get();\n            let min = ctx.min.min(ctx.max);\n            let max = ctx.min.max(ctx.max);\n\n            prop_assert!(value[0].is_finite());\n            prop_assert!(value[1].is_finite());\n            prop_assert!(value[0] >= min - f64::EPSILON);\n            prop_assert!(value[1] <= max + f64::EPSILON);\n            prop_assert!(value[0] <= value[1] + f64::EPSILON);\n\n            for thumb_value in value {\n                let steps = (thumb_value - min) / ctx.step;\n\n                prop_assert!(\n                    (steps - steps.round()).abs() <= 1.0e-9,\n                    \"value {thumb_value} must stay on step grid {min}..{max} step {}\",\n                    ctx.step\n                );\n            }\n\n            let gap = ctx.step * f64::from(ctx.min_steps_between);\n\n            if gap.is_finite() && max - min >= gap {\n                prop_assert!(\n                    value[1] - value[0] + f64::EPSILON >= gap,\n                    \"range {:?} must preserve gap {gap}\",\n                    value\n                );\n            }\n\n            if (ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly) && interactive_mutation {\n                prop_assert_eq!(value, previous);\n            }\n\n            let api = service.connect(&|_| {});\n\n            let start = api.thumb_attrs(range_slider::ThumbIndex::Start);\n            let end = api.thumb_attrs(range_slider::ThumbIndex::End);\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(start.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"slider\"));\n            prop_assert_eq!(end.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"slider\"));\n\n            let start_max = start\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax))\n                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<f64>().ok())\n                .expect(\"start aria-valuemax must parse\");\n\n            let end_min = end\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin))\n                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<f64>().ok())\n                .expect(\"end aria-valuemin must parse\");\n\n            prop_assert!(start_max <= value[1] + f64::EPSILON);\n            prop_assert!(end_min >= value[0] - f64::EPSILON);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ars_components::input::pin_input;\nuse ars_core::{Direction, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\nfn arb_pin_mode() -> impl Strategy<Value = pin_input::Mode> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        Just(pin_input::Mode::Numeric),\n        Just(pin_input::Mode::Alphanumeric),\n        Just(pin_input::Mode::Password),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn arb_pin_input_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = pin_input::Props> {\n    (\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        arb_pin_mode(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(disabled, invalid, otp, mask, required, auto_submit, mode)| pin_input::Props {\n                id: \"pin-input\".to_string(),\n                value: None,\n                default_value: Vec::new(),\n                length: 4,\n                disabled,\n                invalid,\n                otp,\n                mask,\n                placeholder: Some(\"_\".to_string()),\n                mode,\n                name: Some(\"pin\".to_string()),\n                form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                required,\n                readonly: false,\n                select_on_focus: false,\n                blur_on_complete: false,\n                auto_submit,\n                on_value_complete: None,\n                dir: Direction::Ltr,\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_pin_input_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = pin_input::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        (0_usize..4, any::<bool>())\n            .prop_map(|(index, is_keyboard)| pin_input::Event::Focus { index, is_keyboard }),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::Blur),\n        (\n            0_usize..4,\n            \"[0-9a-z]\".prop_map(|s| s.chars().next().unwrap_or('0'))\n        )\n            .prop_map(|(index, c)| pin_input::Event::InputChar { index, char: c }),\n        (0_usize..4).prop_map(|index| pin_input::Event::DeleteChar { index }),\n        \"[0-9a-z]{0,8}\".prop_map(pin_input::Event::Paste),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::Clear),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::FocusNext),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::FocusPrev),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::CompositionStart),\n        Just(pin_input::Event::CompositionEnd),\n    ]\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_pin_input_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_pin_input_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_pin_input_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<pin_input::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &pin_input::Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        for event in events {\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            // Cell vector always has the configured length\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get().len(), ctx.length);\n\n            // Complete iff every cell is non-empty (for length > 0).\n            let all_filled = ctx.length > 0 && ctx.value.get().iter().all(|cell| !cell.is_empty());\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.complete, all_filled);\n\n            // Hidden input value equals the joined cell strings.\n            let hidden_attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).hidden_input_attrs();\n\n            let joined = ctx.value.get().join(\"\");\n\n            prop_assert_eq!(\n                hidden_attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value),\n                Some(joined.as_str())\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

1	//! CheckboxGroup component state machine and connect API.
2	//!
3	//! This module implements the framework-agnostic `CheckboxGroup` machine
4	//! defined in `spec/components/input/checkbox-group.md`. The machine owns a
5	//! `BTreeSet<Key>` checked-value set, group-level accessibility state, select-all
6	//! helpers, child checkbox context, and native form-submission metadata.

119	
120	impl Context {
121	    /// Returns the checked state for a parent checkbox representing all values.
122	    #[must_use]
123	    pub fn parent_checked_state(&self, all_values: &BTreeSet<Key>) -> checkbox::State {
124	        if all_values.is_empty() {
125	            return checkbox::State::Unchecked;
126	        }
127	
128	        let checked_count = all_values
129	            .iter()
130	            .filter(|value| self.value.get().contains(*value))
131	            .count();
132	
133	        if checked_count == 0 {
134	            checkbox::State::Unchecked
135	        } else if checked_count == all_values.len() {
136	            checkbox::State::Checked
137	        } else {
138	            checkbox::State::Indeterminate
139	        }
140	    }
141	
142	    /// Returns whether a specific value is currently checked.
143	    #[must_use]
144	    pub fn is_checked(&self, value: &Key) -> bool {
145	        self.value.get().contains(value)
146	    }
147	
148	    /// Returns true when the maximum number of checked values has been reached.
149	    #[must_use]
150	    pub fn is_at_max(&self) -> bool {
151	        self.max_checked
152	            .is_some_and(|max| self.value.get().len() >= max)
153	    }
154	}

464	    fn init(
465	        props: &Self::Props,
466	        _env: &Env,
467	        _messages: &Self::Messages,
468	    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
469	        (
470	            State::Idle,
471	            Context {
472	                value: if let Some(value) = &props.value {
473	                    Bindable::controlled(clamp_to_max(value.clone(), props.max_checked))
474	                } else {
475	                    Bindable::uncontrolled(clamp_to_max(
476	                        props.default_value.clone(),
477	                        props.max_checked,
478	                    ))
479	                },
480	                name: props.name.clone(),
481	                disabled: props.disabled,
482	                required: props.required,
483	                readonly: props.readonly,
484	                invalid: props.invalid,
485	                dir: props.dir,
486	                orientation: props.orientation,
487	                max_checked: props.max_checked,
488	                focused: false,
489	                focus_visible: false,
490	                has_description: false,
491	                has_error_message: false,
492	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
493	            },
494	        )
495	    }
496	
497	    fn transition(
498	        _state: &Self::State,
499	        event: &Self::Event,
500	        ctx: &Self::Context,
501	        props: &Self::Props,
502	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
503	        if (ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly)
504	            && matches!(
505	                event,
506	                Event::Toggle(_)
507	                    | Event::Check(_)
508	                    | Event::Uncheck(_)
509	                    | Event::CheckAll
510	                    | Event::UncheckAll
511	            )
512	        {
513	            return None;
514	        }
515	
516	        match event {
517	            Event::Toggle(value) => {
518	                let mut next = ctx.value.get().clone();
519	
520	                if !next.remove(value) {
521	                    if ctx.is_at_max() {
522	                        return None;
523	                    }
524	
525	                    next.insert(value.clone());
526	                }
527	
528	                Some(value_change_plan(ctx, next))
529	            }
530	
531	            Event::Check(value) => {
532	                if ctx.value.get().contains(value) {
533	                    return Some(TransitionPlan::new());
534	                }
535	
536	                if ctx.is_at_max() {
537	                    return None;
538	                }
539	
540	                let mut next = ctx.value.get().clone();
541	
542	                next.insert(value.clone());
543	
544	                Some(value_change_plan(ctx, next))
545	            }
546	
547	            Event::Uncheck(value) => {
548	                if !ctx.value.get().contains(value) {
549	                    return Some(TransitionPlan::new());
550	                }
551	
552	                let mut next = ctx.value.get().clone();
553	
554	                next.remove(value);
555	
556	                Some(value_change_plan(ctx, next))
557	            }
558	
559	            Event::SetValue(value) => {
560	                let controlled = props
561	                    .value
562	                    .clone()
563	                    .map(|value| clamp_to_max(value, ctx.max_checked));
564	                let next = controlled
565	                    .clone()
566	                    .unwrap_or_else(|| clamp_to_max(value.clone(), ctx.max_checked));
567	
568	                Some(sync_value_plan(controlled, next))
569	            }
570	
571	            Event::CheckAll => {
572	                let next = clamp_to_max(props.all_values.clone(), ctx.max_checked);
573	                Some(value_change_plan(ctx, next))
574	            }
575	
576	            Event::UncheckAll => Some(value_change_plan(ctx, BTreeSet::new())),
577	
578	            Event::Reset => {
579	                let next = clamp_to_max(props.default_value.clone(), ctx.max_checked);
580	                Some(value_change_plan(ctx, next))
581	            }
582	
583	            Event::Focus { is_keyboard } => {
584	                let is_keyboard = *is_keyboard;
585	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
586	                    ctx.focused = true;
587	                    ctx.focus_visible = is_keyboard;
588	                }))
589	            }
590	
591	            Event::Blur => Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
592	                ctx.focused = false;
593	                ctx.focus_visible = false;
594	            })),
595	
596	            Event::SetProps => {
597	                let controlled = props.value.clone();
598	                let name = props.name.clone();
599	                let disabled = props.disabled;
600	                let required = props.required;
601	                let readonly = props.readonly;
602	                let invalid = props.invalid;
603	                let dir = props.dir;
604	                let orientation = props.orientation;
605	                let max_checked = props.max_checked;
606	
607	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
608	                    let controlled = controlled.map(|value| clamp_to_max(value, max_checked));
609	
610	                    if let Some(value) = &controlled {
611	                        ctx.value.set(value.clone());
612	                    } else {
613	                        ctx.value
614	                            .set(clamp_to_max(ctx.value.get().clone(), max_checked));
615	                    }
616	
617	                    ctx.value.sync_controlled(controlled);
618	                    ctx.name = name;
619	                    ctx.disabled = disabled;
620	                    ctx.required = required;
621	                    ctx.readonly = readonly;

942	fn value_change_plan(ctx: &Context, next: BTreeSet<Key>) -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
943	    if ctx.value.get() == &next {
944	        return TransitionPlan::new();
945	    }
946	
947	    let effect = value_change_effect(next.clone());
948	
949	    if ctx.value.is_controlled() {
950	        return TransitionPlan::new()
951	            .apply(|_: &mut Context| {})
952	            .with_effect(effect);
953	    }
954	
955	    TransitionPlan::new()
956	        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
957	            ctx.value.set(next);
958	        })
959	        .with_effect(effect)
960	}
961	
962	fn sync_value_plan(
963	    controlled: Option<BTreeSet<Key>>,
964	    next: BTreeSet<Key>,
965	) -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
966	    TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
967	        ctx.value.set(next);
968	        ctx.value.sync_controlled(controlled);
969	    })
970	}
971	
972	fn value_change_effect(next: BTreeSet<Key>) -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
973	    PendingEffect::new(Effect::ValueChange, move |_ctx, props: &Props, _send| {
974	        if let Some(callback) = &props.on_change {
975	            callback(next);
976	        }
977	
978	        no_cleanup()
979	    })
980	}
981	
982	fn clamp_to_max(values: BTreeSet<Key>, max_checked: Option<usize>) -> BTreeSet<Key> {
983	    max_checked.map_or(values.clone(), |max| values.into_iter().take(max).collect())
984	}
985	
986	const fn orientation_token(orientation: Orientation) -> &'static str {
987	    match orientation {
988	        Orientation::Horizontal => "horizontal",

19	pub enum Key {
20	    /// String key — the universal fallback.
21	    String(String),
22	
23	    /// Integer key — allocation-free for numeric identifiers.
24	    Int(u64),
25	
26	    /// UUID key — allocation-free for UUID-based identifiers.
27	    ///
28	    /// Available only when the `uuid` feature is enabled. Provides a
29	    /// 16-byte `Copy` key without heap allocation, compared to the 36-byte
30	    /// `String` representation of a UUID.
31	    #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
32	    Uuid(uuid::Uuid),
33	}
34	
35	/// Manual `PartialOrd` / `Ord` implementation: `Int` keys sort before `String`
36	/// keys so that numeric identifiers (common in database-driven collections)
37	/// cluster together at the front of `BTreeSet<Key>` used by `selection::State`.
38	/// Within each variant the natural ordering applies (`u64::cmp` for `Int`,
39	/// lexicographic for `String`).
40	///
41	/// When the `uuid` feature is enabled, the ordering is `Int < Uuid < String`:
42	/// numeric IDs first, then UUIDs (also structured identifiers), then
43	/// arbitrary strings.
44	///
45	/// **Note on mixed-key ordering**: When a collection contains
46	/// both `Key::Int` and `Key::String` keys, all `Int` keys sort before all
47	/// `String` keys. This is intentional for database-backed collections where
48	/// numeric IDs and string IDs are not intermixed. If your use case requires a
49	/// single unified ordering, normalize all keys to `Key::String` (e.g., via
50	/// `Key::str(id.to_string())`). For database-sourced numeric IDs, prefer
51	/// `Key::from_database_id(u64)` (alias for `Key::Int`) to make the sort
52	/// behavior explicit.
53	impl PartialOrd for Key {
54	    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<core::cmp::Ordering> {
55	        Some(self.cmp(other))
56	    }
57	}
58	
59	impl Ord for Key {
60	    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> core::cmp::Ordering {
61	        // Variant ordering: Int (0) < Uuid (1) < String (2).
62	        // Within the same variant: natural ordering.
63	        match (self, other) {
64	            (Key::Int(a), Key::Int(b)) => a.cmp(b),
65	            (Key::String(a), Key::String(b)) => a.cmp(b),
66	            (Key::Int(_), Key::String(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Less,
67	            (Key::String(_), Key::Int(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
68	            #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
69	            (Key::Uuid(a), Key::Uuid(b)) => a.cmp(b),
70	            #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
71	            (Key::Int(_), Key::Uuid(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Less,
72	            #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
73	            (Key::Uuid(_), Key::Int(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
74	            #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
75	            (Key::Uuid(_), Key::String(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Less,
76	            #[cfg(feature = "uuid")]
77	            (Key::String(_), Key::Uuid(_)) => core::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
78	        }
79	    }
80	}
81	
82	impl Key {
83	    /// Construct a string key.
84	    #[must_use]
85	    pub fn str(s: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
86	        Key::String(s.into())
87	    }
88

2	//!
3	//! This module implements the framework-agnostic `RadioGroup` machine defined
4	//! in `spec/components/input/radio-group.md`. The machine owns single-value
5	//! selection, stable-key roving focus, per-item disabled state, and ARIA/form
6	//! metadata while leaving DOM focus resolution to framework adapters.

394	    fn init(
395	        props: &Self::Props,
396	        _env: &Env,
397	        _messages: &Self::Messages,
398	    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
399	        (
400	            State::Idle,
401	            Context {
402	                value: if let Some(value) = &props.value {
403	                    Bindable::controlled(Some(value.clone()))
404	                } else {
405	                    Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone())
406	                },
407	                focused_item: None,
408	                focus_visible: false,
409	                disabled: props.disabled,
410	                readonly: props.readonly,
411	                required: props.required,
412	                invalid: props.invalid,
413	                orientation: props.orientation,
414	                dir: props.dir,
415	                name: props.name.clone(),
416	                loop_focus: props.loop_focus,
417	                has_description: false,
418	                has_error_message: false,
419	                items: Vec::new(),
420	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
421	            },
422	        )
423	    }
424	
425	    fn transition(
426	        state: &Self::State,
427	        event: &Self::Event,
428	        ctx: &Self::Context,
429	        props: &Self::Props,
430	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
431	        match event {
432	            Event::SelectValue(value) => select_value_plan(ctx, Some(value.clone())),
433	
434	            Event::FocusItem { item, is_keyboard } => {
435	                if !can_focus_item(ctx, item) {
436	                    return None;
437	                }
438	
439	                if matches!(state, State::Focused { item: current } if current == item)
440	                    && ctx.focused_item.as_ref() == Some(item)
441	                    && ctx.focus_visible == *is_keyboard
442	                {
443	                    return None;
444	                }
445	
446	                Some(focus_only_plan(item.clone(), *is_keyboard))
447	            }
448	
449	            Event::Blur => {
450	                if matches!(state, State::Idle) && ctx.focused_item.is_none() && !ctx.focus_visible
451	                {
452	                    return None;
453	                }
454	
455	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
456	                    ctx.focused_item = None;
457	                    ctx.focus_visible = false;
458	                }))
459	            }
460	
461	            Event::FocusNext => {
462	                let target = match state {
463	                    State::Focused { item } => step_focus(ctx, item, FocusStep::Next)?,
464	                    State::Idle => idle_focus_seed(ctx, FocusStep::Next)?,
465	                };
466	
467	                Some(focus_and_maybe_select_plan(ctx, target))
468	            }
469	
470	            Event::FocusPrev => {
471	                let target = match state {
472	                    State::Focused { item } => step_focus(ctx, item, FocusStep::Prev)?,
473	                    State::Idle => idle_focus_seed(ctx, FocusStep::Prev)?,
474	                };
475	
476	                Some(focus_and_maybe_select_plan(ctx, target))
477	            }
478	
479	            Event::FocusFirst => {
480	                let target = first_enabled(ctx)?;
481	                Some(focus_and_maybe_select_plan(ctx, target))
482	            }
483	
484	            Event::FocusLast => {
485	                let target = last_enabled(ctx)?;
486	                Some(focus_and_maybe_select_plan(ctx, target))
487	            }
488	
489	            Event::RegisterItem(item) => {
490	                let item = item.clone();
491	                let clears_focus = item.disabled && ctx.focused_item.as_ref() == Some(&item.value);

493	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
494	                } else {
495	                    TransitionPlan::new()
496	                };
497	
498	                Some(plan.apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
499	                    if let Some(existing) = ctx
500	                        .items
501	                        .iter_mut()
502	                        .find(|existing| existing.value == item.value)
503	                    {
504	                        *existing = item;
505	                    } else {
506	                        ctx.items.push(item);
507	                    }
508	
509	                    if clears_focus {
510	                        ctx.focused_item = None;
511	                        ctx.focus_visible = false;
512	                    }

  "content": "use std::collections::BTreeSet;\n\nuse ars_collections::Key;\nuse ars_components::input::checkbox_group;\nuse ars_core::{Direction, Env, Orientation, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\n/// Size of the bounded key pool the strategies draw from. Keeping the universe\n/// small makes membership invariants meaningful and lets sequences revisit the\n/// same keys often.\nconst KEY_POOL: u64 = 6;\n\nfn arb_key() -> impl Strategy<Value = Key> {\n    (0_u64..KEY_POOL).prop_map(Key::Int)\n}\n\nfn arb_key_set() -> impl Strategy<Value = BTreeSet<Key>> {\n    prop::collection::btree_set(arb_key(), 0..=KEY_POOL as usize)\n}\n\nfn arb_checkbox_group_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = checkbox_group::Props> {\n    (\n        arb_key_set(),\n        arb_key_set(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        prop::option::of(0_usize..=KEY_POOL as usize),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(default_value, all_values, disabled, readonly, required, invalid, max_checked)| {\n                checkbox_group::Props {\n                    id: \"checkbox-group\".to_string(),\n                    value: None,\n                    default_value,\n                    name: Some(\"group\".to_string()),\n                    form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                    disabled,\n                    required,\n                    readonly,\n                    invalid,\n                    dir: Direction::Ltr,\n                    orientation: Orientation::Vertical,\n                    all_values,\n                    max_checked,\n                    on_change: None,\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_checkbox_group_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = checkbox_group::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        arb_key().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::Toggle),\n        arb_key().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::Check),\n        arb_key().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::Uncheck),\n        arb_key_set().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::SetValue),\n        Just(checkbox_group::Event::CheckAll),\n        Just(checkbox_group::Event::UncheckAll),\n        Just(checkbox_group::Event::Reset),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| checkbox_group::Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),\n        Just(checkbox_group::Event::Blur),\n        Just(checkbox_group::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::SetHasDescription),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(checkbox_group::Event::SetHasErrorMessage),\n    ]\n}\n\n/// Mirrors `clamp_to_max` in the machine: keep the first `max` keys in `Ord`\n/// order, dropping the rest.\nfn clamp_to_max(values: BTreeSet<Key>, max: Option<usize>) -> BTreeSet<Key> {\n    match max {\n        Some(max) => values.into_iter().take(max).collect(),\n        None => values,\n    }\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_checkbox_group_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_checkbox_group_props(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_checkbox_group_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let max = props.max_checked;\n\n        let mut service = Service::<checkbox_group::Machine>::new(\n            props.clone(),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &checkbox_group::Messages,\n        );\n\n        // The uncontrolled set starts as the clamped default.\n        let mut expected = clamp_to_max(props.default_value.clone(), max);\n\n        prop_assert_eq!(service.context().value.get(), &expected);\n\n        for event in events {\n            // `disabled`/`readonly` reject Toggle/Check/Uncheck/CheckAll/UncheckAll\n            // but NOT SetValue or Reset (the machine guard omits them).\n            let blocked = props.disabled || props.readonly;\n\n            match &event {\n                checkbox_group::Event::Toggle(key) => {\n                    if !blocked {\n                        if expected.contains(key) {\n                            expected.remove(key);\n                        } else if max.is_none_or(|max| expected.len() < max) {\n                            expected.insert(key.clone());\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::Check(key) => {\n                    if !blocked\n                        && !expected.contains(key)\n                        && max.is_none_or(|max| expected.len() < max)\n                    {\n                        expected.insert(key.clone());\n                    }\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::Uncheck(key) => {\n                    if !blocked {\n                        expected.remove(key);\n                    }\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::CheckAll => {\n                    if !blocked {\n                        expected = clamp_to_max(props.all_values.clone(), max);\n                    }\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::UncheckAll => {\n                    if !blocked {\n                        expected.clear();\n                    }\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::SetValue(value) => {\n                    expected = clamp_to_max(value.clone(), max);\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::Reset => {\n                    expected = clamp_to_max(props.default_value.clone(), max);\n                }\n                checkbox_group::Event::Focus { .. }\n                | checkbox_group::Event::Blur\n                | checkbox_group::Event::SetProps\n                | checkbox_group::Event::SetHasDescription(_)\n                | checkbox_group::Event::SetHasErrorMessage(_) => {}\n            }\n\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            // The machine's checked set tracks the model exactly.\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get(), &expected);\n\n            // The checked set never exceeds `max_checked`.\n            if let Some(max) = max {\n                prop_assert!(ctx.value.get().len() <= max);\n            }\n\n            // Parent (select-all) state derives from the children deterministically.\n            let parent = ctx.parent_checked_state(&props.all_values);\n            let checked = props\n                .all_values\n                .iter()\n                .filter(|key| ctx.value.get().contains(*key))\n                .count();\n            let derived = if props.all_values.is_empty() || checked == 0 {\n                checkbox_group::checkbox::State::Unchecked\n            } else if checked == props.all_values.len() {\n                checkbox_group::checkbox::State::Checked\n            } else {\n                checkbox_group::checkbox::State::Indeterminate\n            };\n            prop_assert_eq!(parent, derived);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

1183:fn reset_value_plan(ctx: &Context, next: Option<Key>) -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
        if !can_focus_item(self.ctx, item_value) {
            return "-1";
        }

        if self.is_roving_anchor(item_value) {
            "0"
        } else {
            "-1"
        }
    }

    fn is_roving_anchor(&self, item_value: &Key) -> bool {
        match self.state {
            State::Focused { item } => item == item_value,

            State::Idle => {
                if let Some(selected) = self.selected_value() {
                    return if can_focus_item(self.ctx, selected) {
                        selected == item_value
                    } else {
                        first_enabled(self.ctx).as_ref() == Some(item_value)
                    };
                }

                first_enabled(self.ctx).as_ref() == Some(item_value)
            }
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum FocusStep {
    Next,
    Prev,
}

fn reset_value_plan(ctx: &Context, next: Option<Key>) -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
    value_change_plan(ctx, next)
}

  "content": "use ars_collections::Key;\nuse ars_components::input::radio_group;\nuse ars_core::{Direction, Env, Orientation, Service};\nuse proptest::prelude::*;\n\n/// Bounded key pool the strategies draw from, so navigation and selection\n/// sequences revisit the same items repeatedly.\nconst KEY_POOL: u64 = 5;\n\nfn arb_key() -> impl Strategy<Value = Key> {\n    (0_u64..KEY_POOL).prop_map(Key::Int)\n}\n\nfn arb_radio() -> impl Strategy<Value = radio_group::Radio> {\n    (arb_key(), any::<bool>()).prop_map(|(value, disabled)| radio_group::Radio { value, disabled })\n}\n\n/// A unique-by-key set of radio items rendered into the registry.\nfn arb_items() -> impl Strategy<Value = Vec<radio_group::Radio>> {\n    prop::collection::btree_set(arb_key(), 0..=KEY_POOL as usize)\n        .prop_flat_map(|keys| {\n            let count = keys.len();\n            (Just(keys), prop::collection::vec(any::<bool>(), count))\n        })\n        .prop_map(|(keys, disabled_flags)| {\n            keys.into_iter()\n                .zip(disabled_flags)\n                .map(|(value, disabled)| radio_group::Radio { value, disabled })\n                .collect()\n        })\n}\n\nfn arb_radio_group_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = radio_group::Props> {\n    (\n        prop::option::of(arb_key()),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n        any::<bool>(),\n    )\n        .prop_map(\n            |(default_value, disabled, readonly, required, invalid, loop_focus)| {\n                radio_group::Props {\n                    id: \"radio-group\".to_string(),\n                    value: None,\n                    default_value,\n                    disabled,\n                    readonly,\n                    required,\n                    invalid,\n                    orientation: Orientation::Vertical,\n                    dir: Direction::Ltr,\n                    name: Some(\"group\".to_string()),\n                    form: Some(\"form\".to_string()),\n                    loop_focus,\n                    on_value_change: None,\n                }\n            },\n        )\n}\n\nfn arb_radio_group_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = radio_group::Event> {\n    prop_oneof![\n        arb_key().prop_map(radio_group::Event::SelectValue),\n        (arb_key(), any::<bool>())\n            .prop_map(|(item, is_keyboard)| radio_group::Event::FocusItem { item, is_keyboard }),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::FocusNext),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::FocusPrev),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::FocusFirst),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::FocusLast),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::Blur),\n        arb_radio().prop_map(radio_group::Event::RegisterItem),\n        arb_key().prop_map(radio_group::Event::UnregisterItem),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::Reset),\n        Just(radio_group::Event::SetProps),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(radio_group::Event::SetHasDescription),\n        any::<bool>().prop_map(radio_group::Event::SetHasErrorMessage),\n    ]\n}\n\n/// Whether a key is disabled from the group's point of view: either the whole\n/// group is disabled or the registered item carries its own disabled flag.\nfn item_disabled(ctx: &radio_group::Context, key: &Key) -> bool {\n    ctx.disabled\n        || ctx\n            .items\n            .iter()\n            .any(|item| &item.value == key && item.disabled)\n}\n\nfn is_registered(ctx: &radio_group::Context, key: &Key) -> bool {\n    ctx.items.iter().any(|item| &item.value == key)\n}\n\nproptest! {\n    #![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n    fn proptest_radio_group_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(\n        props in arb_radio_group_props(),\n        items in arb_items(),\n        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_radio_group_event(), 0..128),\n    ) {\n        let mut service = Service::<radio_group::Machine>::new(\n            props,\n            &Env::default(),\n            &radio_group::Messages,\n        );\n\n        // Seed the registry so roving focus has items to traverse.\n        drop(service.send(radio_group::Event::SetItems(items)));\n\n        for event in events {\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            let prev_value = ctx.value.get().clone();\n\n            // Capture pre-send predicates for the SelectValue assertion: the\n            // registry does not change on SelectValue, so these stay valid.\n            let select_target = match &event {\n                radio_group::Event::SelectValue(key) => Some(key.clone()),\n                _ => None,\n            };\n            let group_blocked = ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly;\n            let target_disabled = select_target\n                .as_ref()\n                .is_some_and(|key| item_disabled(ctx, key));\n\n            drop(service.send(event));\n\n            let ctx = service.context();\n\n            // State and `focused_item` stay consistent.\n            match service.state() {\n                radio_group::State::Idle => {\n                    prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused_item.as_ref(), None);\n                }\n                radio_group::State::Focused { item } => {\n                    prop_assert_eq!(ctx.focused_item.as_ref(), Some(item));\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Keyboard focus-visibility cannot outlive a focused item.\n            prop_assert!(!ctx.focus_visible || ctx.focused_item.is_some());\n\n            // Focus only ever rests on a registered, enabled item.\n            if let Some(focused) = ctx.focused_item.clone() {\n                prop_assert!(is_registered(ctx, &focused));\n                prop_assert!(!item_disabled(ctx, &focused));\n            }\n\n            if let Some(key) = select_target {\n                if group_blocked {\n                    // Disabled/read-only groups never change the selection.\n                    prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get(), &prev_value);\n                } else if !target_disabled {\n                    // Selecting an enabled value replaces the prior selection\n                    // (and is idempotent when it was already selected).\n                    prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get().as_ref(), Some(&key));\n                } else {\n                    // Selecting a disabled item is rejected.\n                    prop_assert_eq!(ctx.value.get(), &prev_value);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

=== slider impl error_message_attrs (1166-1180) ===
    pub fn error_message_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ErrorMessage.data_attrs();

        attrs
            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
            .set(part_attr, part_val)
            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("error-message"))
            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), "polite");

        attrs

> AGENT

Here's where the audit stands.

## What's done and verified green

**Test reorganization (the core ask)**
- `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs` (1 file, 274 lines) → `input/mod.rs` + 14 per-component files. All 14 anatomy tests pass; full conformance suite still 93/93.
- `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs` (1 file, 1,403 lines) → `input/mod.rs` + 13 per-component files. Shared strategies (`arb_short_text`, `arb_slider_step`) hoisted into `mod.rs`; everything else lives with its component. `file_trigger` is documented-as-skipped (stateless).
- Both follow the existing `overlay/`/`utility/` per-component layout. No monolith remains (largest input file is now 192 lines).

**New proptests (filling the gaps)**
- `checkbox_group`: model-based — tracks the expected `BTreeSet<Key>` through every event, asserting `max_checked` clamping, disabled/readonly blocking, and the parent select-all derivation (empty/all/mixed → unchecked/checked/indeterminate).
- `radio_group`: structural invariants — State↔`focused_item` consistency, focus only rests on enabled registered items, single-selection replace/idempotency, and disabled/readonly blocking.
- Both pass at the default 1,000 cases.

**Drift found and fixed in-place**
- `radio_group` emitted `role="alert"` on its error message, contradicting its own spec (§1.7 mandates `aria-live="polite"`) and the 8 sibling input components that all use `"polite"`. Fixed impl + regenerated snapshot. (This touches a `.snap`, so the PR will need the `snapshot-reviewed` label.)

**Audit verdict:** all 14 input components are otherwise correctly implemented and spec-faithful. clippy clean, `xtask spec validate` passes (340 files).

## Two things need your call

**1. Pre-existing CI blocker (not mine).** 7 lib tests fail at branch HEAD — `data_display::meter` and `data_display::progress` snapshots expect `"50%"` but the code now emits `"50"`. I confirmed this fails with my changes stashed, so it predates this work and is outside the input category. But it *will* fail `cargo xci-fast`, blocking the push.

**2. `aria-errormessage` cross-component inconsistency.** 6 components (number_input, search_input, password_input, slider, range_slider, pin_input) set `aria-errormessage` on their control when invalid, but **none of their specs mention it** — every spec deliberately documents `aria-describedby`-only. checkbox/switch/text_field/textarea don't set it. So either the 6 impls drifted (add an undocumented attribute) or the specs are incomplete.

403	    pub fn format_percent(&self, value: f64, max_fraction_digits: Option<u8>) -> String {
404	        let mut options = self.options.clone();
405	
406	        options.style = Style::Percent;
407	        options.min_fraction_digits = 0;
408	        options.max_fraction_digits = max_fraction_digits.unwrap_or(0);
409	
410	        Self::new(&self.locale, options).format(value)
411	    }

413	    /// Format a start/end numeric range using a locale-sensitive separator.
414	    #[must_use]
415	    pub fn format_range(&self, start: f64, end: f64, locale: &Locale) -> String {
416	        let separator = match locale.language() {
417	            "fr" => " – ",
418	            "ja" | "zh" | "ko" => "〜",
419	            _ => "–",
420	        };
421	
422	        format!("{}{}{}", self.format(start), separator, self.format(end))
423	    }

425	    #[cfg(any(
426	        feature = "icu4x",
427	        not(all(feature = "web-intl", target_arch = "wasm32"))
428	    ))]
429	    fn prepare_decimal(&self, value: f64) -> Decimal {
430	        let rounded = self.prepare_numeric_value(value, true);
431	
432	        let precision = usize::from(self.options.max_fraction_digits);
433	
434	        let decimal_literal = if precision == 0 {
435	            format!("{rounded:.0}")
436	        } else {
437	            format!("{rounded:.precision$}")
438	        };
439	
440	        let mut decimal = Decimal::from_str(&decimal_literal).unwrap_or_default();
441	
442	        decimal.absolute.trim_end();
443	
444	        decimal
445	            .absolute
446	            .pad_end(-i16::from(self.options.min_fraction_digits));
447	
448	        decimal
449	            .absolute
450	            .pad_start(i16::from(self.options.min_integer_digits.get()));
451	
452	        decimal.apply_sign_display(self.options.sign_display.into_fixed_decimal());
453	
454	        decimal
455	    }
456

465	            value * 100.0
466	        } else {
467	            value
468	        };

749	        "CLF" | "UYW" => 4,

752	        | "DJF" | "KMF" | "VUV" | "PYG" => 0,
753

Key correction: `xci-fast` runs the unit gate with `--all-features` (line 312), so these 7 tests **pass in the real gate** — they only fail under plain default-feature `cargo test`. ```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/audit-input-components/crates/ars-i18n/src/number/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "fn fallback_format(value: f64, formatter: &Formatter) -> String {\n    let mut output = formatter.prepare_decimal(value).to_string();\n\n    if formatter.decimal_separator != '.' {\n        output = output.replace('.', &String::from(formatter.decimal_separator));\n    }\n\n    output\n}",
  "new_string": "fn fallback_format(value: f64, formatter: &Formatter) -> String {\n    let mut output = formatter.prepare_decimal(value).to_string();\n\n    if formatter.decimal_separator != '.' {\n        output = output.replace('.', &String::from(formatter.decimal_separator));\n    }\n\n    // The minimal fallback formatter intentionally omits locale-specific\n    // grouping and the currency/unit affixes that the ICU4X and web-`Intl`\n    // backends apply. The percent sign is the one affix it does append, because\n    // a percentage rendered without it (`\"50\"` instead of `\"50%\"`) is not just\n    // less precise — it changes meaning, which matters for `aria-valuetext`\n    // exposed by components such as `meter` and `progress` in no-ICU4X builds.\n    if matches!(formatter.options.style, Style::Percent) {\n        output.push('%');\n    }\n\n    output\n}"
}
```

350:    pub fn thumb_attrs(&self, thumb: ThumbIndex) -> AttrMap;
===== range-slider a11y table region (480-495) =====

## 4. Internationalization

- Same locale resolution as Slider.
- Thumb labels ("Range start", "Range end") localized via `Messages`.
- Output format: "50 – 80" — en-dash and number formatting per locale.
- RTL: Arrow keys reverse for horizontal orientation. Thumb positions mirror visually.

## 5. Form Integration

- **Hidden inputs**: Two hidden `<input type="hidden">` elements are rendered — one per thumb. They carry `name[0]` and `name[1]` with the start and end values.
- **Validation states**: `aria-invalid` can be set on the Root if needed by wrapping in a Field.
- **Reset behavior**: On form reset, the adapter restores values to `default_value`.
- **Disabled propagation**: When inside a `Field` or `Fieldset`, the adapter merges `disabled` from `FieldCtx` per `07-forms.md` §12.6.
- **Geometry boundary**: Core pointer helpers accept adapter-supplied pointer and
  track geometry. The core never reads DOM layout, resolves DOM IDs to elements,
```

  "new_string": "    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Truncates `values` to at most `max_checked` keys (in `Ord` order). Every\n/// value-origination path (init, `SetValue`, `CheckAll`, `Reset`) runs the\n/// incoming set through this helper so the group can never hold more than\n/// `max_checked` checked values — including the initial controlled or\n/// uncontrolled set.\nfn clamp_to_max(values: BTreeSet<Key>, max_checked: Option<usize>) -> BTreeSet<Key> {\n    match max_checked {\n        Some(max) => values.into_iter().take(max).collect(),\n        None => values,\n    }\n}\n```"
