```
Perfect. Now I can see the pattern clearly across all 7 components. Let me verify my understanding by confirming the key pattern in the Part Mapping tables - all the ARIA attributes come from the `api.*_attrs()` methods, not hardcoded:

Based on my thorough review of all 14 adapter spec files (Leptos and Dioxus for each of the 7 components), here is the audit verdict:

## Audit Report: Adapter Spec ARIA Attribute Changes

All 7 adapter specifications consistently delegate to core `Api` methods for ARIA attributes rather than hardcoding them. No adapter changes are needed.

**Verdict for each component:**

1. **radio_group** (role/aria-live change): Adapter specs delegate to `api.error_message_attrs()` — no hardcoded `role="alert"` or `aria-live` found anywhere. Both Leptos and Dioxus specs reference the error-message part's attributes solely through the API method.

2. **number_input** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs reference `api.input_attrs()` for the Input part. The Part Mapping table (line 52/66) clearly states "Attr source: `api.input_attrs()`" with no hardcoded attribute enumeration. Aria-errormessage will flow from core automatically.

3. **search_input** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs use `api.input_attrs()` exclusively. Part Mapping specifies the attribute source as the API method, never listing individual attributes.

4. **password_input** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs delegate to `api.input_attrs()`. No hardcoded ARIA attributes appear in the specifications.

5. **slider** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs use `api.thumb_attrs()` for the Thumb part. Part Mapping states attrs come from the API method. The thumb owns `role="slider"` semantics through the API, and aria-errormessage will propagate automatically.

6. **range_slider** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs reference `api.thumb_attrs(thumb)` for per-thumb attributes. The Part Mapping table (line 59/74) shows attrs come from the API method, not hardcoded enumerations.

7. **pin_input** (aria-errormessage addition): Adapter specs use `api.input_attrs(index)` for cell inputs. Attributes are delegated to the API method throughout both frameworks.

**Key finding:** All adapter specs follow the same pattern: Part Mapping tables reference `api.*_attrs()` methods (e.g., `api.error_message_attrs()`, `api.input_attrs()`, `api.thumb_attrs()`), and implementation sketches use `.derive(|api| api.method()).get()` (Leptos) or `.read().clone()` (Dioxus). No adapter spec hardcodes individual ARIA attributes like `role="alert"`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-live`, or `aria-errormessage` in code examples or text. The core spec changes are transparent to adapters.

**Recommendation:** No adapter spec updates required. The Leptos and Dioxus adapter specs are framework-agnostic regarding the specific ARIA attributes used—they correctly delegate all attribute construction to the core Api layer.
```

The audit is complete and everything is green. Here's the consolidated result.

## What changed

**Test reorganization (the core ask) — verified behavior-identical**
- `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs` → `input/mod.rs` + 14 per-component files (93 conformance tests pass, 14 input anatomy among them).
- `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs` (1,403 lines) → `input/mod.rs` + 13 per-component files; shared `arb_short_text`/`arb_slider_step` hoisted to `mod.rs`; `file_trigger` documented-as-skipped (stateless). 96 proptests pass with `--include-ignored`.

**New proptests**: `checkbox_group` (model-based: set tracking, `max_checked` clamp, parent-state derivation) and `radio_group` (structural: State↔focus consistency, focus-lands-on-enabled, select replace/idempotency, disabled blocking).

**Drift fixes landed**
- `radio_group` error message: `role="alert"` → `aria-live="polite"` (its own spec mandated polite; all 8 siblings use it). Snapshot regenerated → **PR needs the `snapshot-reviewed` label**.
- `ars-i18n` `fallback_format`: now appends `%` for `Style::Percent`. This was the root cause of the 7 pre-existing `data_display` meter/progress failures (`aria-valuetext="50"` instead of `"50%"` in no-icu4x builds) — a real a11y bug, not just a test issue. Added `fallback_format_appends_percent_sign`. All 3453 lib tests now pass under default features.

**Spec documentation (resolving real drift)**
- `aria-errormessage` documented in 6 specs (number-input, search-input, password-input, slider, range-slider, pin-input) — aligns with the **blessed foundation convention** at `03-accessibility.md:1210` (aria-errormessage + describedby fallback). Fixed range-slider's §5 prose that contradicted the impl.
- Spec gaps closed: pin-input `dir` field, search-input `debounce_pending`, checkbox-group `max_checked` clamping (added a `clamp_to_max` helper applied across init/SetValue/CheckAll/Reset/SetProps).

**Verification**: clippy clean (ars-components default+all-features; ars-i18n icu4x/web-intl/fallback); `xtask spec validate` (340 files) + `compile-snippets` (2,171 blocks) green; no snapshot orphans. Mutation testing skipped per your earlier decision.

**Out of scope (flagged, not changed)**: pin-input's spec uses string effect names (`PendingEffect::new("complete", …)`) vs the impl's typed `Effect` enum — but that's a **corpus-wide** spec convention (slider etc. do the same), not a pin-input gap, so modernizing one spec would create inconsistency.

One genuine a11y design decision surfaced by the audit, plus the issue/commit gate:

```
{"body":"## Goal\n\nImplement the framework-agnostic core for all 14 input components: state machines, ConnectApi implementations, Props/Api/Part types, and attribute/event mapping.\n\n## Scope\n\nAll components in `spec/components/input/`:\n\n**Stateless (1):** FileTrigger\n\n**Stateful (11):** Checkbox, CheckboxGroup, Editable, NumberInput, PasswordInput, PinInput, RadioGroup, SearchInput, Switch, TextField, Textarea\n\n**Complex (2):** Slider, RangeSlider\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Framework-specific adapter components (Leptos/Dioxus) — tracked under Epics #8/#9.\n- Other component categories.\n\n## Spec refs\n\n- `spec/components/input/_category.md`\n- `spec/components/input/*.md` (14 component specs)\n- `spec/foundation/07-forms.md`\n\n## Decomposition\n\n12 tasks organized in dependency waves:\n\n**Wave 1 — No dependencies (10 tasks, 37 pts):** FileTrigger (2), Checkbox (3), Switch (3), TextField+Textarea (5), PasswordInput+SearchInput (5), NumberInput (5), PinInput (5), RadioGroup (5), Editable (5), Slider (5)\n\n**Wave 2 — After Wave 1 (2 tasks, 11 pts):** CheckboxGroup (3, after Checkbox), RangeSlider (8, after Slider)\n\n**Total: 48 story points across 12 tasks.**\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- All 14 input components have agnostic core implementations.\n- Each component's public types are documented per workspace `missing_docs` lint.\n- State machines (stateful components) match their spec §1 exactly.\n- ConnectApi implementations produce correct ARIA attributes per spec §2.\n- Form integration (hidden inputs, validation, field context) matches spec for all input components.\n- All tests pass with zero warnings.\n- Spec and implementation remain aligned; any mismatch is resolved in the same task.\n\n## Spec impact\n\nAny implementation/spec mismatch must be fixed in the same task.","number":220,"state":"OPEN","title":"epic: Agnostic input components"}
```

Everything is complete and green. Final state for your review before I commit anything.

## Final change set (30 files + 2 new test dirs)

**Test reorganization** — `spec_conformance/input.rs` (274 lines) → 15 files; `proptest_state_machines/input.rs` (1,403 lines) → 14 files. Net −1,708/+220 lines on those, plus the per-component files.

**Code fixes**
- `radio_group/mod.rs`: `role="alert"` → `aria-live="polite"` (spec contract + sibling consistency)
- `checkbox/switch/text_field/textarea/mod.rs`: added `aria-errormessage` on invalid (+ unit assertions)
- `ars-i18n/number/mod.rs`: fallback formatter appends `%` for percentages (+ test) — fixes the 7 pre-existing `data_display` failures at root

**New tests**: `checkbox_group` + `radio_group` proptests; `fallback_format_appends_percent_sign`; 6 new explicit `aria-errormessage` unit assertions.

**Specs** (11 files): `aria-errormessage` documented across all 10 error-message-bearing input components (now consistent with the foundation convention); pin-input `dir`, search-input `debounce_pending`, checkbox-group `max_checked` clamping documented.

**9 snapshots** regenerated (so the PR needs the `snapshot-reviewed` label).

**Verification (all green)**: 3,453 lib + 93 conformance + 96 proptests; ars-i18n icu4x (317) / fallback (162); clippy clean (ars-components all-features, ars-i18n ×3 backends); `xtask spec validate` (340) + `compile-snippets` (2,171). Mutation testing skipped per your earlier decision.

Nothing is committed yet. The PR will target `main` with `Closes #220`.

```json
  "command": "cd /Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/audit-input-components; gh pr create --title \"Audit input components: per-component test split + spec/a11y sync\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nFinal category-wide audit of the agnostic input components, closing the epic. No new component logic — this verifies all 14 input components against their specs, reorganizes their tests per-component, and lands every drift finding in one PR.\n\n### Test reorganization\n- Split the monolithic `tests/spec_conformance/input.rs` (274 lines) and `tests/proptest_state_machines/input.rs` (1,403 lines) into per-component modules under `input/`, matching the `overlay/` and `utility/` layout. Shared proptest strategies (`arb_short_text`, `arb_slider_step`) hoisted into `input/mod.rs`; `file_trigger` documented-as-skipped (stateless).\n- Added state-machine proptests for the two components that lacked them: `checkbox_group` (model-based set tracking, `max_checked` clamp, parent select-all derivation) and `radio_group` (State↔focus consistency, focus-lands-on-enabled, single-selection replace/idempotency, disabled blocking).\n\n### Drift fixes\n- **radio_group**: error message now uses `aria-live=\"polite\"` (was `role=\"alert\"`), matching its own spec and all sibling input components.\n- **checkbox / switch / text_field / textarea**: now emit `aria-errormessage` on invalid, aligning every error-message-bearing input component with the foundation a11y convention (`foundation/03-accessibility.md`).\n- **ars-i18n fallback formatter**: appends `%` for `Style::Percent`. The fallback previously dropped the suffix, producing semantically-wrong `aria-valuetext=\"50\"` (should be `\"50%\"`) in no-ICU4X builds — the root cause of pre-existing `data_display` meter/progress snapshot failures under default features.\n\n### Spec synchronization\n- Documented `aria-errormessage` across all 10 error-message-bearing input specs (code examples + ARIA tables).\n- Documented previously-undocumented impl behavior: pin-input `dir` field, search-input `debounce_pending` context field, checkbox-group `max_checked` clamping; aligned the switch spec's `Effect` enum with the impl.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `cargo xci-fast` — all 10 gates pass (fmt, check, clippy, unit, integration, adapter, spec-compile-snippets, error-variant-coverage, mutual-exclusion, snapshot-count)\n- [x] `cargo test -p ars-components` — 3,453 lib + 93 conformance pass\n- [x] `cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --include-ignored` — 96 proptests pass (13 input, incl. 2 new)\n- [x] `cargo test -p ars-i18n` — icu4x (317) + fallback (162, incl. new percent test) pass\n- [x] `cargo clippy` clean across ars-components (all-features) and ars-i18n (icu4x / web-intl / fallback)\n- [x] `cargo xtask spec validate` (340 files) + `spec compile-snippets` (2,171 blocks) pass\n\nMutation testing intentionally skipped (refactor + audit, not new-component delivery; the changed components already passed the mutation gate on original delivery).\n\nCloses #220\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
```
